function v18198ClubRows(s,f){

  for(const [key,rows] of Object.entries(db||{})){
   const parts=String(key).split(':'),kind=parts[0]||'',country=parts[1]||'',division=parts[2]||'';
   const wanted=String(f.kind||'all');

   if(wanted!=='all'&&wanted!==kind&&wanted!==key)continue;

  }
  return map;
 }

function v18198Merge(map,r,national=false){

  let x=map.get(r.id);
  if(!x){x={id:r.id,name:r.name||state?.players?.[r.id]?.name||r.id,natCode:r.natCode||state?.players?.[r.id]?.natCode||'UNK',goals:0,assists:0,apps:0,minutes:0,clubs:{},nationalTeams:{}};map.set(r.id,x);}
  x.name=r.name||x.name;x.natCode=r.natCode||x.natCode;
  x.goals+=Number(r.goals)||0;x.assists+=Number(r.assists)||0;x.apps+=Number(r.apps)||0;x.minutes+=Number(r.minutes)||0;
  if(national){if(r.teamCode)x.nationalTeams[r.teamCode]=r.teamName||r.teamCode;}
  else Object.assign(x.clubs,r.clubs||{});
 }

function v18203CollectRows(){
  const s=state;if(!s)return [];
  const f=(typeof scorerFilter!=='undefined'&&scorerFilter)||{};
  const selected=f.year==='all'?'all':Number(f.year||s.year);
  const map=new Map(),seen=new WeakSet();
  const merge=(raw,key='')=>{
   if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object')return;
   const goals=v18203Number(raw.goals??raw.goal??raw.g??raw.stats?.goals);
   const assists=v18203Number(raw.assists??raw.assist??raw.a??raw.stats?.assists);
   const apps=v18203Number(raw.apps??raw.appearances??raw.matches??raw.games??raw.j??raw.stats?.apps);
   const minutes=v18203Number(raw.minutes??raw.mins??raw.stats?.minutes);
   if(!goals&&!assists&&!apps&&!minutes)return;
   const player=raw.player&&typeof raw.player==='object'?raw.player:null;
   const rawId=raw.id??raw.playerId??raw.player_id??player?.id??key;
   const id=String(rawId||'');
   const live=id?s.players?.[id]:null;
   const rawName=raw.name??raw.playerName??raw.player_name??player?.name??live?.name??id;
   const name=rawName||'Atleta';
   const natCode=raw.natCode??raw.nationality??player?.natCode??live?.natCode??'UNK';
   const mapKey=id||(v18203Norm(name)+'|'+natCode);
   let row=map.get(mapKey);
   if(!row){row={id,name,natCode,goals:0,assists:0,apps:0,minutes:0,clubs:{}};map.set(mapKey,row);}
   row.goals+=goals;row.assists+=assists;row.apps+=apps;row.minutes+=minutes;
   if(raw.clubs&&typeof raw.clubs==='object')Object.assign(row.clubs,raw.clubs);
   const clubName=raw.teamName??raw.clubName??raw.team?.name??raw.club?.name;
   if(clubName)row.clubs[String(raw.teamId??raw.clubId??Object.keys(row.clubs).length)]=clubName;
  };
  const walk=(node,key='',depth=0)=>{
   if(node==null||depth>6||typeof node!=='object')return;
   if(seen.has(node))return;seen.add(node);
   if(Array.isArray(node)){for(const item of node)walk(item,'',depth+1);return;}
   if(node instanceof Map){for(const [k,v] of node.entries())walk(v,String(k),depth+1);return;}
   const looks=('goals' in node)||('goal' in node)||('assists' in node)||('apps' in node)||('appearances' in node)||('matches' in node)||('minutes' in node)||('stats' in node);
   if(looks){merge(node,key);return;}
   for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(node))walk(v,k,depth+1);
  };
  const archives=Array.isArray(s.scorerArchive)?s.scorerArchive:[];
  if(selected==='all'){
   walk(s.allTimeStats||{});
   if(!map.size){for(const archive of archives)walk(archive?.competitions||archive);walk(s.competitionStats||{});}
  }else{
   const archived=archives.find(item=>Number(item?.year)===selected);
   const live=s.competitionStats||{};
   const liveRows=Object.values(live).some(value=>value&&typeof value==='object'&&Object.keys(value).length);
   if(selected!==Number(s.year)||(archived&&(!liveRows||s.seasonDone)))walk(archived?.competitions||archived||{});
   else walk(live);
  }
  try{
   const national=s.v640National?.playerStats;
   if(national){if(selected==='all')walk(national);else walk(national?.[String(selected)]||national?.[selected]||{});}
  }catch(e){}
  return [...map.values()].filter(row=>row.goals>0).sort((a,b)=>b.goals-a.goals||b.assists-a.assists||a.minutes-b.minutes||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'pt-BR'));
 }

function v3ApplyMatch(s,m){
    const r=baseApplyMatchV2(s,m);
    for(const side of m.sides){
      const t=team(s,side.id),key=statKey(s,m,t),bucket=s.competitionStats[key]||(s.competitionStats[key]={});
      for(const [id,mins] of Object.entries(side.minutes)){
        if(mins<=0)continue;const p=player(s,id),row=statRow(s,bucket,p,t),all=allTimeExisting125(s,p,t);
        row.apps++;row.minutes+=mins;if(row.byClub){row.byClub[t.id].apps++;row.byClub[t.id].minutes+=mins;}
        if(all){all.apps++;all.minutes+=mins;if(all.byClub){all.byClub[t.id].apps++;all.byClub[t.id].minutes+=mins;}}
      }
      const ix=m.sides.indexOf(side);
      for(const event of m.events.filter(e=>e.type==='goal'&&e.side===ix)){
        const p=player(s,event.pid),row=statRow(s,bucket,p,t),all=allTimeRow125(s,p,t);
        row.goals++;all.goals++;if(row.byClub)row.byClub[t.id].goals++;if(all.byClub)all.byClub[t.id].goals++;
        if(event.assist&&player(s,event.assist)){
          const a=player(s,event.assist),ar=statRow(s,bucket,a,t),aa=allTimeRow125(s,a,t);ar.assists++;aa.assists++;if(ar.byClub)ar.byClub[t.id].assists++;if(aa.byClub)aa.byClub[t.id].assists++;
        }
      }
    }
    if(m.comp==='world'&&r.winner){
      const winner=team(s,r.winner),round=s.schedule[s.day].round;
      ledger(s,winner,'Mundial de Clubes',[650000,1400000,2800000,8000000][round],'Classificação / título');
      if(round===3){s.worldWinner=r.winner;news(s,'O campeão do mundo',winner.name+' conquistou o Mundial de Clubes.','good');}
    }
    recordCoachMatch(s,m,r);return r;
  }

function infoboxFields(text){
 const start=text.search(/\{\{\s*(?:Infobox football biography|Info\/Futebolista|Info\/Treinador|Infobox football manager)\b/i);if(start<0)return {};
 const fields={};let depth=0,link=0,part='',begun=false;
 const store=()=>{const m=part.match(/^\s*([^=\n]+)\s*=([^]*)$/);if(m){const k=n(m[1]).replace(/ /g,''),v=m[2].trim();fields[k]=fields[k]&&fields[k]!==v?'':v;}part='';};
 for(let i=start;i<text.length;i++){const c=text.slice(i,i+2);
  if(c==='{{'){depth++;if(begun)part+=c;i++;continue;}
  if(c==='}}'){depth--;if(depth===0){store();break;}if(begun)part+=c;i++;continue;}
  if(c==='[['){link++;if(begun)part+=c;i++;continue;}if(c===']]'){link=Math.max(0,link-1);if(begun)part+=c;i++;continue;}
  if(text[i]==='|'&&depth===1&&!link){if(begun)store();begun=true;continue;}if(begun)part+=text[i];
 }
 return fields;
}
function coachCandidate(html,lang){
 const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html'),box=doc.querySelector('table.infobox');if(!box)return null;
 const candidates=[];
 for(const row of box.querySelectorAll('tr')){if(row.closest('table')!==box)continue;const label=row.querySelector('th'),cell=row.querySelector('td');if(!label||!cell)continue;
  const header=n(label.textContent.replace(/\[[^\]]*\]/g,''));if(!/^(manager|head coach|headcoach|coach|tecnico|treinador|treinador a|treinador principal|tecnico principal)$/.test(header))continue;
  const c=cell.cloneNode(true);c.querySelectorAll('sup,.reference,del,s,script,style').forEach(x=>x.remove());
  const links=[...c.querySelectorAll('a[href]')].map(a=>({title:wikiLink(a.getAttribute('href'),lang),name:a.textContent.trim()})).filter(x=>x.title&&x.name.length>=2);
  const unique=[...new Map(links.map(x=>[titleKey(x.title),x])).values()];
  if(unique.length!==1)return null;const item=unique[0];if(/assistant|auxiliar|president|director|diretor/i.test(c.textContent))return null;
  candidates.push({...item,interim:/interim|caretaker|interino|provisorio/i.test(n(c.textContent))});
 }
 return candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:null;
}
function managerCurrent(text,t,title,asOf){
 const f=infoboxFields(text),cf=f.currentclub||f.clubeatual||f.equipeatual||'',keys=new Set([title,t.name,...(t.aliases||[])].map(clubKey));
 const matches=v=>{const links=[...String(v).matchAll(/\[\[([^\]|]+)(?:\|[^\]]+)?\]\]/g)].map(x=>x[1]);return(links.length?links:[clean(v).replace(/\([^)]*\)/g,'')]).some(x=>keys.has(clubKey(x)));};
 let open=[],future=false,uncertain=false,endedHere=false;
 for(const[k,value]of Object.entries(f)){const m=k.match(/^(?:managerclubs|clubescomotreinador|clubestreinador|treinadorclubes)(\d+)$/);if(!m)continue;
  const y=f['manageryears'+m[1]]||f['anoscomotreinador'+m[1]]||f['anostreinador'+m[1]]||f['treinadoranos'+m[1]]||'';
  const years=clean(y).replace(/[\u2010-\u2015\u2212]/g,'-').trim();
  const active=years.match(/^(\d{4})\s*-\s*(present|current|atual|atualidade|presente)?\s*$/i);
  if(active){if(Number(active[1])>Number(asOf.slice(0,4))){if(matches(value))future=true;continue;}open.push({value,year:active[1]});}
  else if(matches(value)&&/^\d{4}\s*-\s*\d{4}$/.test(years))endedHere=true;
  else if(matches(value)&&(!years||!/\d{4}\s*-\s*\d{4}/.test(years)))uncertain=true;
 }
 if(future)return{status:'future'};
 if(open.length>1)return{status:'conflict'};
 if(open.length&&(!matches(open[0].value)||cf&&!matches(cf)))return{status:'conflict'};
 const role=/\b(manager|head coach|coach|tecnico|treinador|caretaker|interim|interino)\b/.test(n(clean(cf)));
 if(open.length===1&&matches(open[0].value))return{status:'confirmed',interim:/interim|caretaker|interino/.test(n(open[0].value+' '+cf))};
 if(cf&&role&&endedHere)return{status:'conflict'};
 if(cf&&role&&!uncertain)return{status:matches(cf)?'confirmed':'conflict',interim:/interim|caretaker|interino/.test(n(cf))};
 return{status:'unknown'};
}
async function createFeed(options){
 const {clubs,signal,onProgress=()=>{},wikiTitles={},natCode=()=> 'UNK',cache={version:VERSION,clubs:{}},saveCache=async()=>{},fetchImpl=root.fetch.bind(root)}=options;

 if(!batch.length)throw Error('Nenhum clube selecionado.');if(batch.length>1500)throw Error('Selecione uma associacao ou as grandes ligas para limitar a consulta.');
 let lastRequest=0,failures=0;const entityCache=new Map();const store=cache.version===VERSION&&cache.clubs&&typeof cache.clubs==='object'?cache:{version:VERSION,clubs:{}};
 async function request(site,params){
  check(signal);const url=new URL(API[site]);url.search=new URLSearchParams({format:'json',formatversion:'2',origin:'*',maxlag:'5',...params}).toString();
  for(let attempt=0;attempt<3;attempt++){
   await delay(Math.max(0,300-(Date.now()-lastRequest)),signal);lastRequest=Date.now();const ctrl=new AbortController(),stop=()=>ctrl.abort(),timer=setTimeout(stop,20000);signal?.addEventListener('abort',stop,{once:true});
   let wait=0;
   try{
    const r=await fetchImpl(url.href,{signal:ctrl.signal,credentials:'omit',referrerPolicy:'no-referrer',redirect:'error',headers:{'Api-User-Agent':'FutBRManager/18.151 (https://futbrmanager.com.br; roster updater)'}});
    if(r.status===429||r.status===503){const h=r.headers.get('Retry-After'),seconds=Number(h),stamp=Date.parse(h||'');wait=h?(Number.isFinite(seconds)?seconds*1000:Math.max(0,stamp-Date.now())):1500*2**attempt;if(!Number.isFinite(wait))wait=3000;if(wait>60000||attempt===2)throw Error('Wikimedia temporariamente ocupada. Tente novamente mais tarde.');await r.body?.cancel();}
    else{
     if(!r.ok)throw Error('Wikimedia HTTP '+r.status);if(Number(r.headers.get('Content-Length')||0)>8*1024*1024)throw Error('Resposta acima do limite seguro.');
     const reader=r.body?.getReader();let raw='',size=0;if(reader){const dec=new TextDecoder();for(;;){const z=await reader.read();if(z.done)break;size+=z.value.byteLength;if(size>8*1024*1024){await reader.cancel();throw Error('Resposta acima do limite seguro.');}raw+=dec.decode(z.value,{stream:true});}raw+=dec.decode();}else raw=await r.text();
     const j=JSON.parse(raw);if(j.error?.code==='maxlag'||j.error?.code==='ratelimited'){if(attempt===2)throw Error('Wikimedia ocupada; consulta adiada.');wait=1500*2**attempt;}else if(j.error)throw Error('Wikimedia: '+String(j.error.code).slice(0,60));else return j;
    }
   }catch(e){check(signal);if(e.name==='AbortError')throw Error('Tempo limite ao consultar Wikimedia.');throw e;}
   finally{clearTimeout(timer);signal?.removeEventListener('abort',stop);}if(wait)await delay(wait,signal);
  }
 }

}

/* FutBR 18.152: deterministic palette extraction from incorporated crest pixels. */
(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;else root.FutBRCrestPalette=api;})(typeof globalThis==='object'?globalThis:this,function(){
'use strict';
const hex=c=>'#'+c.map(x=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(x))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
const valid=c=>typeof c==='string'&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c);
const rgb=c=>[1,3,5].map(i=>parseInt(c.slice(i,i+2),16));
const lin=x=>{x/=255;return x<=.04045?x/12.92:((x+.055)/1.055)**2.4;};
function lab(c){const [r,g,b]=c.map(lin),f=x=>x>.008856?Math.cbrt(x):7.787*x+16/116;const X=f((.4124564*r+.3575761*g+.1804375*b)/.95047),Y=f(.2126729*r+.7151522*g+.072175*b),Z=f((.0193339*r+.119192*g+.9503041*b)/1.08883);return [116*Y-16,500*(X-Y),200*(Y-Z)];}
const dist=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1],a[2]-b[2]);
function luminance(c){const a=rgb(c).map(lin);return .2126*a[0]+.7152*a[1]+.0722*a[2];}
function contrast(a,b){const x=luminance(a),y=luminance(b);return (Math.max(x,y)+.05)/(Math.min(x,y)+.05);}
function ink(p){const base=p[0];for(const c of p.slice(1))if(valid(c)&&contrast(base,c)>=4.5)return c;return contrast(base,'#000000')>=contrast(base,'#ffffff')?'#000000':'#ffffff';}
function signature(s){let n=2166136261;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++){n^=s.charCodeAt(i);n=Math.imul(n,16777619);}return s.length+':'+(n>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0');}
function extract(image,preferred){
 const hints=Array.isArray(preferred)?preferred:[preferred];
 const {data,width:w,height:h}=image||{};
 if(!data||!Number.isInteger(w)||!Number.isInteger(h)||w<4||h<4||w>512||h>512||data.length!==w*h*4)return null;
 let minX=w,minY=h,maxX=-1,maxY=-1,count=0;
 for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){if(data[(y*w+x)*4+3]<210)continue;count++;minX=Math.min(minX,x);maxX=Math.max(maxX,x);minY=Math.min(minY,y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y);}
 if(count<24)return null;
 let exterior=null;
 if(count>w*h*.97){
  const corners=[0,w-1,(h-1)*w,w*h-1];
  if(corners.every(k=>Math.min(data[k*4],data[k*4+1],data[k*4+2])>=243)){
   exterior=new Uint8Array(w*h);const queue=new Int32Array(w*h);let head=0,tail=0;
   const add=k=>{if(k<0||k>=w*h||exterior[k])return;const i=k*4;if(Math.min(data[i],data[i+1],data[i+2])<243)return;exterior[k]=1;queue[tail++]=k;};
   for(let x=0;x<w;x++){add(x);add((h-1)*w+x);}for(let y=0;y<h;y++){add(y*w);add(y*w+w-1);}
   while(head<tail){const k=queue[head++],x=k%w;if(x)add(k-1);if(x+1<w)add(k+1);add(k-w);add(k+w);}
   if(tail>w*h*.60||tail>w*h-.18*count)exterior=null;
  }
 }
 const bins=new Map();let weight=0;
 for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
  const at=y*w+x,i=at*4;if(data[i+3]<210||exterior?.[at])continue;
  const c=[data[i],data[i+1],data[i+2]],key=(c[0]>>4)*256+(c[1]>>4)*16+(c[2]>>4);
  const edge=x===minX||x===maxX||y===minY||y===maxY||data[(at-1)*4+3]<210||data[(at+1)*4+3]<210||data[(at-w)*4+3]<210||data[(at+w)*4+3]<210;
  let variation=0,neighbours=0;
  for(const k of [x>0?at-1:-1,x+1<w?at+1:-1,y>0?at-w:-1,y+1<h?at+w:-1]){if(k<0||data[k*4+3]<210)continue;variation+=Math.hypot(c[0]-data[k*4],c[1]-data[k*4+1],c[2]-data[k*4+2]);neighbours++;}
  const flat=neighbours>=3&&variation/neighbours<24,vote=(edge?.28:1)*(flat?1:.15);weight+=vote;
  let b=bins.get(key);if(!b){b={key,n:0,sum:[0,0,0],samples:new Map()};bins.set(key,b);}b.n+=vote;for(let k=0;k<3;k++)b.sum[k]+=vote*c[k];
  const value=(c[0]<<16)|(c[1]<<8)|c[2];b.samples.set(value,(b.samples.get(value)||0)+vote);
 }
 if(weight<24)return null;
 const quantized=[...bins.values()].map(b=>{const c=b.sum.map(x=>x/b.n);let best=null,n=-1;for(const[k,v]of b.samples){if(v>n||(v===n&&k<best)){best=k;n=v;}}return {n:b.n,c,lab:lab(c),hex:hex([best>>16,(best>>8)&255,best&255]),key:b.key};}).sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n||a.key-b.key);
 const clusters=[];
 for(const b of quantized){let target=null,best=22;for(const c of clusters){const d=dist(c.seed,b.lab);if(d<best){best=d;target=c;}}if(target){target.n+=b.n;target.bins.push(b);}else clusters.push({seed:b.lab,n:b.n,bins:[b],hex:b.hex});}
 const sorted=clusters.filter(c=>c.n>=Math.max(8,weight*.018)).sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n||a.hex.localeCompare(b.hex));
 const interpolated=c=>{const v=rgb(c.hex);for(let i=0;i<sorted.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<sorted.length;j++){
  const a=sorted[i],b=sorted[j];if(a===c||b===c||a.n/weight<.055||b.n/weight<.055||Math.max(a.n,b.n)<c.n*1.15)continue;
  const x=rgb(a.hex),d=rgb(b.hex).map((n,k)=>n-x[k]),l=d.reduce((n,k)=>n+k*k,0);if(l<90*90)continue;
  const t=v.reduce((n,k,z)=>n+(k-x[z])*d[z],0)/l;if(t<=.12||t>=.88)continue;
  if(Math.hypot(...v.map((n,k)=>n-x[k]-t*d[k]))<32)return true;
 }return false;};
 const ranked=sorted.filter(c=>!interpolated(c));
 if(!ranked.length)return null;
 let primary=ranked[0];
 if(valid(hints[0])){
  const ref=lab(rgb(hints[0]));let near=null,best=48;
  for(const c of ranked){if(c.n/weight<.07||c.n<ranked[0].n*.20)continue;const d=dist(ref,c.seed);if(d<best){best=d;near=c;}}
  if(near&&near.n/weight>=.07&&near.n>=ranked[0].n*.20)primary=near;
 }
 const picked=[primary];
 const chroma=c=>{const v=rgb(c),lo=Math.min(...v),hi=Math.max(...v);return (hi-lo)/Math.max(hi,1);};
 const different=(a,b)=>{
  if(dist(a.seed,b.seed)<24)return false;
  const x=rgb(a.hex),y=rgb(b.hex),na=chroma(a.hex)<.16,nb=chroma(b.hex)<.16;
  if(na&&nb)return contrast(a.hex,b.hex)>=6;
  if(!na&&!nb){const hue=v=>{const mx=Math.max(...v),mn=Math.min(...v),d=mx-mn;return ((mx===v[0]?(v[1]-v[2])/d:mx===v[1]?(v[2]-v[0])/d+2:(v[0]-v[1])/d+4)*60+360)%360;};const dh=Math.abs(hue(x)-hue(y));if(Math.min(dh,360-dh)<22&&contrast(a.hex,b.hex)<3)return false;}
  return true;
 };
 for(const hint of hints.slice(1,3)){
  if(!valid(hint))continue;const ref=lab(rgb(hint));let near=null,best=34;
  for(const c of ranked){if(picked.includes(c)||c.n/weight<.03||!picked.every(p=>different(c,p)))continue;const d=dist(ref,c.seed);if(d<best){best=d;near=c;}}
  if(near)picked.push(near);
 }
 for(const c of ranked){if(picked.length===3)break;if(c===primary||c.n/weight<(picked.length===1?.04:.045))continue;if(picked.every(p=>different(c,p)))picked.push(c);}
 const colors=picked.map(x=>x.hex);if(colors.length===1)colors.push(ink(colors));while(colors.length<3)colors.push(colors[0]);
 return {colors,ink:ink(colors),coverage:picked.reduce((a,c)=>a+c.n,0)/weight,groups:ranked.slice(0,8).map(c=>({hex:c.hex,share:Math.round(c.n/weight*1000)/1000})),pixels:count};
}
return Object.freeze({extract,ink,contrast,luminance,signature,valid,version:'18.152'});
});

function advanceDay(s){
    if(s.seasonDone)throw Error('Inicie uma nova temporada.');prepareStage(s);const stage=s.schedule[s.day];const own=upcoming(s);if(own&&(!s.live||s.live.status!=='finished'))throw Error('Conclua sua partida antes de avançar.');
    const matches=[];
    for(const f of stage.fixtures){if(f.result)continue;const m=(s.live&&s.live.fixtureId===f.id)?s.live:finishMatch(s,newMatch(s,f,stage.type));matches.push({f,m});}
    // Recover existing absences before applying this day's new cards and injuries.
    s.teams.forEach(t=>trainAndRecover(s,t,matches.some(x=>x.m.home===t.id||x.m.away===t.id)));
    for(const {f,m} of matches){f.result=applyMatch(s,m);for(const side of m.sides){const t=team(s,side.id);for(const [id,minutes] of Object.entries(side.minutes)){if(minutes>0){const p=player(s,id);p.fitness=clamp(side.energy[id]+19+t.medicalLevel*1.7+(t.training==='fitness'?10:0),12,100);if(p.injury)p.fitness=Math.min(p.fitness,60);}}}}
    s.teams.forEach(t=>{stageFinances(s,t,stage.fixtures.some(f=>f.home===t.id||f.away===t.id));if(t.id!==s.user)autoLineup(s,t);else repairLineup(s,t);});
    const userF=stage.fixtures.find(f=>f.home===s.user||f.away===s.user);if(userF){const r=userF.result,won=r.winner===s.user;news(s,won?'Vitória para guardar':r.winner?'Hora de reagir':'Ponto de equilíbrio',team(s,userF.home).name+' '+r.home+' x '+r.away+' '+team(s,userF.away).name+(r.pens?' (pênaltis '+r.pens[0]+' x '+r.pens[1]+')':'')+'.',won?'good':r.winner?'warn':'info');s.board=clamp(s.board+(won?3:r.winner?-3:0)+(human(s).balance<0?-2:0),5,100);}
    if(!userF&&stage.type==='cup')news(s,'A copa segue','As partidas de '+stage.label.replace('Copa Nacional - ','')+' foram disputadas. Seu elenco aproveitou a folga para recuperar o físico.');
    s.lastDay=s.day;s.day++;s.live=null;aiMarket(s);if(s.day>=s.schedule.length)completeSeason(s);else prepareStage(s);return stage;
  }
  function completeSeason(s){
    s.seasonDone=true;const ranking=table(s),t=human(s),position=ranking.findIndex(x=>x.id===s.user)+1;
    ranking.forEach((club,i)=>{ledger(s,club,'Premiação da liga',Math.round(10000000/Math.pow(i+1,.58)),'Classificação final: '+(i+1));});
    const champion=ranking[0],cup=team(s,s.cupWinner),scorer=Object.values(s.players).filter(p=>p.goals).sort((a,b)=>b.goals-a.goals||b.assists-a.assists)[0];if(champion.id===s.user)s.totalTitles++;if(s.cupWinner===s.user)s.totalTitles++;
    s.history.unshift({year:s.year,position,club:t.name,champion:champion.name,cup:cup?cup.name:'-',points:t.stats.pts,gf:t.stats.gf,ga:t.stats.ga,scorer:scorer?scorer.name:'-',goals:scorer?scorer.goals:0,balance:t.balance});s.history=s.history.slice(0,30);
    s.board=clamp(s.board+(position<=t.target?12:-12),5,100);news(s,'Temporada encerrada',champion.name+' é campeão da liga. '+t.name+' termina em '+position+' lugar, com '+t.stats.pts+' pontos. A premiação já entrou no caixa.',position<=t.target?'good':'warn');
  }
  function oldNextSeason(s){
    if(!s.seasonDone||s.live)throw Error('A temporada atual ainda não terminou.');s.year++;const departed=[],retired=[];for(const id of s.free){if(s.players[id])player(s,id).age++;}

    }

    setupSeason(s);news(s,'Uma nova temporada',s.year+' começa.   }
  function upgradeCost(t,type){if(type==='stadium')return Math.round(1800000+t.capacity*43);const level=t[type+'Level'];return Math.round(({training:1800000,youth:1400000,medical:1300000}[type])*Math.pow(1.6,level-1));}
  function upgrade(s,type){if(s.live)throw Error('Conclua a partida.');const t=human(s);if(!['stadium','training','youth','medical'].includes(type))throw Error('Melhoria inválida.');if(type==='stadium'&&t.capacity>=65000)throw Error('Capacidade máxima atingida.');if(type!=='stadium'&&t[type+'Level']>=5)throw Error('Nível máximo atingido.');const cost=upgradeCost(t,type);if(t.balance<cost)throw Error('Caixa insuficiente.');ledger(s,t,'Infraestrutura',-cost,type);if(type==='stadium')t.capacity=Math.min(65000,t.capacity+4000);else t[type+'Level']++;news(s,'Clube em evolução','Investimento em '+({stadium:'estádio',training:'centro de treinamento',youth:'categorias de base',medical:'departamento médico'}[type])+' concluído.','good');return cost;}
  function promoteYouth(s,pos){if(s.live)throw Error('Conclua a partida.');const t=human(s);if(!['GOL','ZAG','LAT','VOL','MEI','ATA'].includes(pos))throw Error('Posição inválida.');if(t.promoted>=2)throw Error('Você já promoveu dois jovens nesta temporada.');if(t.roster.length>=60)throw Error('Seu elenco atingiu o limité de 60 atletas.');const fee=280000+t.youthLevel*60000;if(t.balance<fee)throw Error('Caixa insuficiente.');const id=createPlayer(s,pos,56+t.youthLevel*2+rand(s,0,5),rand(s,17,19));const p=player(s,id);p.potential=clamp(overall(p)+rand(s,13,22)+t.youthLevel,75,95);p.contract=4;p.wage=Math.round(value(p)*.0025/100)*100;t.roster.push(id);p.clubId=t.id;localizeGenerated(s,p,t.country);t.promoted++;ledger(s,t,'Formação',-fee,p.name);news(s,'Da base para o profissional',p.name+' foi promovido.   function baseValidate(s){
    const err=()=>{throw Error('Arquivo de carreira inválido ou incompatível.');};
    if(!s||s.version!==VERSION||!Array.isArray(s.teams)||s.teams.length!==400||!s.players||typeof s.players!=='object'||!Array.isArray(s.free)||!Array.isArray(s.schedule)||s.schedule.length!==48||!Array.isArray(s.news)||!Array.isArray(s.history)||!Number.isInteger(s.day)||s.day<0||s.day>48||!Number.isInteger(s.nextId)||!Number.isInteger(s.year)||!Number.isFinite(s.seed)||typeof s.manager!=='string')err();
    const scan=(x,depth=0)=>{if(depth>16)err();if(typeof x==='number'&&(!Number.isFinite(x)||Math.abs(x)>1e14))err();if(typeof x==='string'&&x.length>5000)err();if(x&&typeof x==='object'){for(const k of Object.keys(x)){if(['__proto__','constructor','prototype'].includes(k))err();scan(x[k],depth+1);}}};scan(s);
    if(Object.keys(s.players).length>35000||s.news.length>100||s.history.length>40)err();const teamIds=new Set(),owners=new Set();
    for(const t of s.teams){if(!/^t\d+$/.test(t.id)||teamIds.has(t.id)||typeof t.name!=='string'||typeof t.short!=='string'||!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(t.color)||!Array.isArray(t.roster)||t.roster.length>90||!Array.isArray(t.lineup)||t.lineup.length!==11||!FORMATIONS[t.tactics?.formation]||!t.stats||!Array.isArray(t.stats.form)||!Array.isArray(t.ledger)||!Number.isFinite(t.balance)||!Number.isFinite(t.capacity))err();teamIds.add(t.id);for(const id of t.roster){if(owners.has(id)||!s.players[id])err();owners.add(id);}for(const id of t.lineup)if(id!==null&&!t.roster.includes(id))err();}
    if(!teamIds.has(s.user))err();for(const id of s.free){if(owners.has(id)||!s.players[id])err();owners.add(id);}
    for(const [id,p] of Object.entries(s.players)){if(!owners.has(id)||!/^p\d+$/.test(id)||p.id!==id||typeof p.name!=='string'||!['GOL','ZAG','LAT','VOL','MEI','ATA'].includes(p.pos)||!p.skills||!Number.isFinite(p.age)||!Number.isFinite(p.fitness)||!Number.isFinite(p.wage))err();for(const k of ['att','def','pas','pace','gk'])if(!Number.isFinite(p.skills[k]))err();}
    for(const d of s.schedule){if(!['league','cup','world'].includes(d.type)||!Array.isArray(d.fixtures))err();for(const f of d.fixtures){if(!teamIds.has(f.home)||!teamIds.has(f.away)||f.home===f.away||typeof f.id!=='string')err();if(f.result&&(!Number.isInteger(f.result.home)||!Number.isInteger(f.result.away)))err();}}
    if(s.live){const m=s.live;if(!['ready','first','halftime','second','finished'].includes(m.status)||!Array.isArray(m.sides)||m.sides.length!==2||!Number.isInteger(m.minute)||m.minute<0||m.minute>90||!Array.isArray(m.events))err();for(const side of m.sides){if(!teamIds.has(side.id)||!Array.isArray(side.lineup)||side.lineup.length!==11||!FORMATIONS[side.tactics?.formation]||!side.energy||!side.minutes||!Array.isArray(side.bench)||!Array.isArray(side.injuries))err();for(const id of side.lineup.filter(Boolean))if(!s.players[id])err();}}
    return s;
  }
  function v4_unpack(text){if(typeof text!=='string'||text.length>160000000)throw Error('O arquivo deve ter menos de 160 MB.');let raw;try{raw=JSON.parse(text);}catch(e){throw Error('Não foi possível ler o JSON da carreira.');}if(!raw||raw.format!=='GESTOR_FC'||raw.version!==VERSION||!raw.data||typeof raw.data!=='object'||checksum(JSON.stringify(raw.data))!==raw.checksum)throw Error('Este não é um backup valido do FutBR Manager.');return validate(raw.data);}

  function table(s,c=human(s).country,division=human(s).division){return s.teams.filter(t=>t.country===c&&t.division===Number(division)).sort((a,b)=>b.stats.pts-a.stats.pts||b.stats.w-a.stats.w||(b.stats.gf-b.stats.ga)-(a.stats.gf-a.stats.ga)||b.stats.gf-a.stats.gf||a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'pt-BR'));}
  function v200ApplyDatabaseOVR(p,target){
    target=clamp(Math.round(Number(target)||0),35,95);if(!p?.skills||!target)return p?overall(p):0;
    const order={GOL:['gk','def','pas','pace','att'],ZAG:['def','pas','pace','att'],LAT:['pace','def','pas','att'],VOL:['pas','def','pace','att'],MEI:['pas','att','pace','def'],ATA:['att','pace','pas','def']}[p.pos]||['att','def','pas','pace'];
    let now=overall(p),delta=target-now;
    if(delta){for(const k of order)p.skills[k]=clamp((Number(p.skills[k])||50)+delta,10,96);now=overall(p);}
    let guard=0;while(now!==target&&guard++<48){const dir=target>now?1:-1,before=Math.abs(target-now);let best=null;
      for(const k of order){const old=Number(p.skills[k])||0,next=clamp(old+dir,10,96);if(next===old)continue;p.skills[k]=next;const test=overall(p);p.skills[k]=old;const dist=Math.abs(target-test);if(dist<before&&(!best||dist<best.dist))best={k,next,test,dist};}
      if(!best)break;p.skills[best.k]=best.next;now=best.test;
    }
    delete p.attributeSignature;p.editorOvr=target;p.editorOvrApplied=target;p.databaseOvr=target;p.potential=Math.max(Number(p.potential)||target,target);return overall(p);
  }

  function roundRobin(ids){
    let wheel=[...ids],rounds=[];for(let r=0;r<19;r++){const games=[];for(let i=0;i<10;i++){let home=wheel[i],away=wheel[19-i];if((r+i)%2)[home,away]=[away,home];games.push({home,away});}rounds.push(games);wheel=[wheel[0],wheel[19],...wheel.slice(1,19)];}
    return rounds.concat(rounds.map(games=>games.map(f=>({home:f.away,away:f.home}))));
  }

##########################################################################################
function v3ApplyMatch(s,m){
    const r=baseApplyMatchV2(s,m);
    for(const side of m.sides){
      const t=team(s,side.id),key=statKey(s,m,t),bucket=s.competitionStats[key]||(s.competitionStats[key]={});
      for(const [id,mins] of Object.entries(side.minutes)){
        if(mins<=0)continue;const p=player(s,id),row=statRow(s,bucket,p,t),all=allTimeExisting125(s,p,t);
        row.apps++;row.minutes+=mins;if(row.byClub){row.byClub[t.id].apps++;row.byClub[t.id].minutes+=mins;}
        if(all){all.apps++;all.minutes+=mins;if(all.byClub){all.byClub[t.id].apps++;all.byClub[t.id].minutes+=mins;}}
      }
      const ix=m.sides.indexOf(side);
      for(const event of m.events.filter(e=>e.type==='goal'&&e.side===ix)){
        const p=player(s,event.pid),row=statRow(s,bucket,p,t),all=allTimeRow125(s,p,t);
        row.goals++;all.goals++;if(row.byClub)row.byClub[t.id].goals++;if(all.byClub)all.byClub[t.id].goals++;
        if(event.assist&&player(s,event.assist)){
          const a=player(s,event.assist),ar=statRow(s,bucket,a,t),aa=allTimeRow125(s,a,t);ar.assists++;aa.assists++;if(ar.byClub)ar.byClub[t.id].assists++;if(aa.byClub)aa.byClub[t.id].assists++;
        }
      }
    }
    if(m.comp==='world'&&r.winner){
      const winner=team(s,r.winner),round=s.schedule[s.day].round;
      ledger(s,winner,'Mundial de Clubes',[650000,1400000,2800000,8000000][round],'Classificação / título');
      if(round===3){s.worldWinner=r.winner;news(s,'O campeão do mundo',winner.name+' conquistou o Mundial de Clubes.','good');}
    }
    recordCoachMatch(s,m,r);return r;
  }

function completeSeason(s){
    awardDomestic(s);baseCompleteSeasonV2(s);
    s.worldHistory[0].worldWinner=s.worldWinner;
    if(s.history[0])s.history[0].worldWinner=team(s,s.worldWinner)?.name||'-';
    const me=s.coaches[s.userCoachId];s.totalTitles=me.titles.filter(x=>!x.key.startsWith('promotion:')).length;
    const archive={year:s.year,competitions:{}};
    for(const [key,rows] of Object.entries(s.competitionStats))archive.competitions[key]=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(rows).filter(([,r])=>r.goals>0||r.assists>0||r.apps>0).map(([k,r])=>[k,copy(r)]));
    s.scorerArchive.unshift(archive);s.scorerArchive=s.scorerArchive.slice(0,10);
    const all=rankingRows(s,{kind:'all'});
    const stars=Object.values(s.players).filter(p=>p.star).length;let slots=Math.max(0,16-stars);
    for(const row of all.slice(0,10)){
      const p=player(s,row.id);if(!p||p.star)continue;
      if(row.goals>=30&&overall(p)>=85)p.starYears=(p.starYears||0)+1;else p.starYears=0;
      if(p.starYears>=2&&slots>0){p.star=true;slots--;news(s,'Uma nova estrela',p.name+' conquistou status de estrela por duas temporadas de elite.','good');}
    }
    if(s.vacation){s.vacation.active=false;s.vacation.paused=true;s.vacation.reason='Temporada concluída.';}
  }

function v4_validate(s){

    const clubs=new Set(s.teams.map(t=>t.id)),owned=new Set(),coaches=new Set();if(clubs.size!==s.teams.length||!clubs.has(s.user)||!s.coaches[s.userCoachId])throw Error('Clubes ou treinador invalidos.');
    for(const t of s.teams){if(!Array.isArray(t.roster)||t.roster.length<11||t.roster.length>60||!Array.isArray(t.academy)||t.academy.length>30||!FORMATIONS[t.tactics?.formation]||!Number.isFinite(t.balance)||t.lineup.length!==11)throw Error('Elenco ou financas invalidos.');for(const id of [...t.roster,...t.academy]){const p=s.players[id];if(!p||p.clubId!==t.id||owned.has(id))throw Error('Vinculo duplicado ou perdido: '+id);owned.add(id);}const c=s.coaches[t.coachId];if(!c||c.clubId!==t.id||coaches.has(c.id))throw Error('Treinador inconsistente.');coaches.add(c.id);if(t.lineup.some(id=>id&&!t.roster.includes(id)))throw Error('Escalacao invalida.');}
    for(const id of s.free){if(!s.players[id]||owned.has(id)||s.players[id].clubId)throw Error('Mercado livre invalido.');owned.add(id);}
    for(const [id,p] of Object.entries(s.players)){if(!owned.has(id)||id!==p.id||!p.skills||!Object.values(p.skills).every(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>=0&&n<=100)||!Number.isFinite(p.wage)||!Number.isInteger(p.age)||!ROLES.includes(p.pos)||!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(p.natCode||''))throw Error('Atleta invalido: '+id);}
    for(const d of s.schedule){const seen=new Set();if(!Array.isArray(d.fixtures))throw Error('Rodada invalida.');for(const f of d.fixtures){if(!clubs.has(f.home)||!clubs.has(f.away)||f.home===f.away||seen.has(f.home)||seen.has(f.away))throw Error('Conflito de partidas.');seen.add(f.home);seen.add(f.away);if(f.result&&(!Number.isInteger(f.result.home)||!Number.isInteger(f.result.away)||f.result.home<0||f.result.away<0))throw Error('Placar invalido.');}}
    if(!s.competitionStats||!s.allTimeStats||!Array.isArray(s.scorerArchive)||!Array.isArray(s.transfers))throw Error('Historico invalido.');return s;
  }

function archiveSeason547(s){
    if(!s?.seasonDone)return false;s.v538CareerArchive??={};if(s.v538CareerArchive[s.year])return false;
    const byPlayer=new Map(),teamNames=new Map((s.teams||[]).map(t=>[t.id,t.name]));
    const row=(pid,tid,name)=>{if(!pid||!tid)return null;let pm=byPlayer.get(pid);if(!pm){pm=new Map();byPlayer.set(pid,pm);}let r=pm.get(tid);if(!r){r={year:Number(s.year),teamId:tid,teamName:name||teamNames.get(tid)||'Clube nao identificado',apps:0,goals:0,assists:0,yellow:0,red:0};pm.set(tid,r);}return r;};
    const fallback=new Map();
    for(const db of Object.values(s.competitionStats||{}))for(const [pid,r] of Object.entries(db||{})){
      if(!r)continue;const bc=r.byClub&&typeof r.byClub==='object'?Object.entries(r.byClub):[];
      if(bc.length){for(const [tid,st] of bc){const x=row(pid,tid,st?.teamName||r.clubs?.[tid]);if(!x)continue;x.apps+=Number(st?.apps)||0;x.goals+=Number(st?.goals)||0;x.assists+=Number(st?.assists)||0;}continue;}
      let f=fallback.get(pid);if(!f){f={apps:0,goals:0,assists:0,cands:new Set()};fallback.set(pid,f);}f.apps+=Number(r.apps)||0;f.goals+=Number(r.goals)||0;f.assists+=Number(r.assists)||0;for(const tid of Object.keys(r.clubs||{}))f.cands.add(tid);
    }
    const lastClub=new Map();for(const m of s.transfers||[])if(Number(m.year)===Number(s.year)&&m.id){if(m.from)row(m.id,m.from,m.fromName);if(m.to){row(m.id,m.to,m.toName);lastClub.set(m.id,m.to);}}
    // 5.50: pertencer a um clube faz parte da carreira mesmo sem entrar em campo.
    // O clube atual e todos os clubes de origem/destino das transferencias da temporada ganham uma linha, ainda que zerada.
    for(const p of Object.values(s.players||{}))if(p?.id&&p.clubId&&p.squad!=='youth')row(p.id,p.clubId,teamNames.get(p.clubId));
    for(const [pid,f] of fallback){const p=s.players?.[pid],c=[...f.cands],owner=p?.clubId||null,tid=(owner&&c.includes(owner)?owner:null)||(lastClub.get(pid)&&c.includes(lastClub.get(pid))?lastClub.get(pid):null)||(c.length===1?c[0]:owner||lastClub.get(pid)||c.at(-1));if(!tid)continue;const x=row(pid,tid);x.apps+=f.apps;x.goals+=f.goals;x.assists+=f.assists;}
    let fixtures=0,cardEvents=0;for(const st of s.schedule||[])for(const f of st.fixtures||[]){fixtures++;const liveEvents=f.result?.events;if(Array.isArray(liveEvents)){for(const e of liveEvents){if((e.type!=='yellow'&&e.type!=='red')||!e.pid)continue;const tid=e.side===0?f.home:e.side===1?f.away:null,x=row(e.pid,tid);if(!x)continue;cardEvents++;if(e.type==='yellow')x.yellow++;else x.red++;}}else for(const c of f.result?.cardIds125||[]){const pid=c?.[0],side=Number(c?.[1]),isRed=!!c?.[2],tid=side===0?f.home:side===1?f.away:null,x=row(pid,tid);if(!x)continue;cardEvents++;if(isRed)x.red++;else x.yellow++;}}
    const season={};for(const [pid,map] of byPlayer){const a=[...map.values()];if(a.length)season[pid]=a;}
    s.v538CareerArchive[s.year]=season;s.v547ArchiveMeta={year:s.year,players:Object.keys(season).length,fixtures,cardEvents,at:Date.now(),worker:typeof document==='undefined'};return true;
  }

  await v18117WaitSyncAck(startSeq);
  for(const key of collections){
   const value=world[key]||(['teams','schedule'].includes(key)?[]:{}),isArray=Array.isArray(value),originalLength=isArray?value.length:0,limit=key==='players'?520:key==='competitionStats'?1:key==='schedule'?2:key==='teams'?250:260;
   self.postMessage({kind:'vac-sync-collection-start',session,key,isArray,length:originalLength});if(key==='players')await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,35));let rows=[],ids=[],done=0,chunk=0;
   const flush=async()=>{if(!rows.length)return;const payload=rows,drop=ids;
    const syncSeq=++v18117SyncSeq;
    if(key==='players')v171PostRows('vac-sync-part',{session,key,done,syncSeq},payload);else self.postMessage({kind:'vac-sync-part',session,key,rows:payload,done,syncSeq});
    if(checkpointMeta){try{await v93RawWrite(v93CheckpointKey(checkpointMeta.token,key,chunk),payload);chunk++;}catch(e){checkpointMeta=null;self.postMessage({kind:'vac-checkpoint-error',session,error:e?.message||String(e)});}}
    rows=[];ids=[];if(destructive&&key==='players')for(const id of drop)delete value[id];
    // Backpressure: cada bloco espera a UI processar, em vez de empilhar
    // milhares de jogadores clonados na fila de mensagens do navegador.
    await v18117WaitSyncAck(syncSeq);
   };
   for(const id in value){if(!safe(id))continue;rows.push([id,value[id]]);ids.push(id);done++;if(rows.length>=limit)await flush();}await flush();
   if(checkpointMeta)checkpointMeta.collections[key]={isArray,length:originalLength,chunks:chunk};
   self.postMessage({kind:'vac-sync-collection-end',session,key,done});if(destructive)world[key]=null;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
  }
  if(checkpointMeta){try{await v93RawWrite(V93_VAC_META,checkpointMeta);checkpointOK=true;if(oldMeta?.token&&oldMeta.token!==checkpointMeta.token){/* 17.1: old checkpoint chunks may back the previous sharded save; cleanup happens after manifest promotion on the UI. */}}catch(e){self.postMessage({kind:'vac-checkpoint-error',session,error:e?.message||String(e)});}}
  // Historical career archives are immutable during vacation and stay mounted in the UI.
  if(destructive)world.v538CareerArchive=null;
  self.postMessage({kind:'vac-sync-end',session,day:finalDay,year:finalYear,checkpoint:checkpointOK,checkpointToken:checkpointMeta?.token||null});
 }

##########################################################################################
FUNCAO COM ESTATISTICAS: simulate @ 9947317

function simulate(message){
  if(running)throw Error('Uma simulação já está ativa.');
  session=message.session;visible=message.visible!==false;delay=Number(message.delay)||600;pause=null;revision=0;running=true;
  const fastManualDay=message.mode==='dayfast', nationalJump=message.mode==='nationaljump', nextMatchJump=message.mode==='nextmatch';
  if(!fastManualDay&&!nationalJump&&!nextMatchJump&&v150ManualSavePromise)await v150ManualSavePromise.catch(()=>{});
  // 18.89: um Worker residente so pode ser reutilizado se tambem estiver
  // sincronizado com a selecao e com a proxima Data FIFA. Dia e ano sozinhos
  // nao garantem identidade de carreira (especialmente apos criacao/convocacao).
  const context=message.nationalContext||{};
  const residentNationalValid=!nationalJump||!!(world?.v640National?.teams?.[String(context.code||'').toUpperCase()]&&
   String(world.v640National.userTeam||'').toUpperCase()===String(context.code||'').toUpperCase()&&
   Number(world.schedule?.length)===Number(context.scheduleLength)&&
   world.schedule.findIndex((d,i)=>i>=Number(world.day||0)&&d?.type==='national')===Number(context.nextNationalDay));
  const nextMatchContext=message.nextMatchContext||{};
  const residentNextMatchValid=!nextMatchJump||!!(String(world?.user||'')===String(nextMatchContext.clubId||'')&&Number(world?.schedule?.length)===Number(nextMatchContext.scheduleLength)&&world?.userEmployed!==false);
  // 18.103: anos, jornadas e ate o proprio clube se repetem entre carreiras novas.
  // Uma identidade exclusiva impede reutilizar o Worker ou o disco de outra carreira.
  const expectedCareerId=String(message.v1903CareerId||'');
  const residentCareerValid=!!(expectedCareerId&&String(world?.v1903CareerId||'')===expectedCareerId&&
    String(world?.user||'')===String(message.v1903User||'')&&
    !!world?.v640National?.userTeam===!!message.v1903National);
  const residentManual=!!((fastManualDay||nationalJump||nextMatchJump||message.mode==='day')&&message.useResident&&world&&Number(world.day)===Number(message.day)&&Number(world.year)===Number(message.year)&&residentNationalValid&&residentNextMatchValid&&residentCareerValid);
  if(!residentManual&&message.loadSaved){
   const stored=await read(STORE);
   if(!stored)throw Error('Ainda nao existe jornada salva para processar.');
   world=await decode(stored);
  }else if(!residentManual&&!message.loadSaved)world=message.state;
  if(!world)throw Error('Estado da carreira indisponivel no Worker.');
  if(expectedCareerId&&(String(world.v1903CareerId||'')!==expectedCareerId||
     String(world.user||'')!==String(message.v1903User||'')||
     !!world?.v640National?.userTeam!==!!message.v1903National))
    throw Error('Estado de outra carreira encontrado no Worker ou salvamento. O avanço foi bloqueado sem alterar a carreira atual.');
  if(nationalJump){
   const context=message.nationalContext||{},code=String(context.code||'').toUpperCase(),root=world.v640National;
   if(!code||!root?.teams?.[code])throw Error('A selecao comandada pelo tecnico nao foi encontrada no estado de simulacao.');
   if(String(root.userTeam||'').toUpperCase()!==code||Number(world.day)!==Number(context.day)||Number(world.year)!==Number(context.year))throw Error('O salvamento e a carreira de selecao exibida estao dessincronizados. Salve a carreira antes de repetir o avanço.');
   if(world.seasonDone)throw Error('A temporada ja terminou. Inicie a proxima temporada para continuar.');
   const nextNationalDay=(world.schedule||[]).findIndex((d,i)=>i>=Number(world.day||0)&&d?.type==='national');
   if(nextNationalDay<0||nextNationalDay!==Number(context.nextNationalDay)||world.schedule.length!==Number(context.scheduleLength))throw Error('O calendario FIFA do Worker diverge do calendario exibido. O avanço foi bloqueado antes de simular jornadas.');
   // Avancar ate a selecao e comando MANUAL. Nunca deve herdar um periodo de
   // ferias ativo, pois isso autorizaria a IA a disputar o jogo do usuario.
   if(world.vacation){world.vacation.active=false;world.vacation.paused=true;}
  }
  if(nextMatchJump){
   const ctx=message.nextMatchContext||{};
   if(!ctx.clubId||String(world.user)!==String(ctx.clubId)||world.userEmployed===false)throw Error('O clube do treinador mudou. Revise a carreira antes de continuar.');
   if(Number(world.day)!==Number(ctx.day)||Number(world.year)!==Number(ctx.year)||Number(world.schedule?.length)!==Number(ctx.scheduleLength))throw Error('O calendario do clube nao corresponde ao salvamento atual. O avanco foi interrompido com seguranca.');
   if(world.seasonDone)throw Error('A temporada esta encerrada. Inicie a proxima para continuar.');
   if(world.live)throw Error('Conclua ou retome a partida ja iniciada antes de avancar.');
   if(world.vacation?.active)throw Error('Encerre as ferias antes do avanco manual para a proxima partida.');
  }
  // 9.3: a second vacation continues from the exact chunked state returned by the
  // previous vacation instead of reloading the older full save.
  if(message.mode==='vacation'&&message.vacationCheckpoint){try{await v93ApplyVacationCheckpoint(message.year,message.day);}catch(e){throw Error('Não foi possível retomar o checkpoint das férias: '+(e?.message||e));}}
  // 9.4: the UI balance is authoritative at the exact moment a vacation block starts.
  // This prevents a stale full-save base from rolling the user's cash backwards between blocks.
  if(message.mode==='vacation'&&Number.isFinite(Number(message.vacationBalance))){const ht=E.team(world,world.user);if(ht)ht.balance=Number(message.vacationBalance);}
  // v81: the club ranking cache belongs to the UI only; never duplicate it inside the Worker.
  if(world.v81ClubRank)delete world.v81ClubRank;
  // v57: authoritative and idempotent human post-match overlay. The UI snapshot
  // captured at the final whistle is the source of truth for this one consolidation.
  // If a previous attempt already advanced this exact day, do NOT advance twice: just
  // republish the resident state and close the stale local live session.
  let v157HumanOverlay=false,v157AlreadyConsolidated=false,v157HumanDay=null;
  if(fastManualDay&&message.manualOverlay){
   const o=message.manualOverlay,expected=Number(o.day),current=Number(world.day);
   if(!Number.isFinite(expected))throw Error('Jornada da partida finalizada indisponivel.');
   v157HumanDay=expected;
   if(current===expected+1){v157AlreadyConsolidated=true;}
   else{
    if(current!==expected)throw Error('A carreira e a partida finalizada estao em jornadas diferentes. Recarregue o ultimo save antes de continuar.');
    const live=o.live;
    if(!live||live.status!=='finished')throw Error('O apito final nao chegou ao consolidar da rodada. Retome a partida e conclua novamente.');
    const stage=world.schedule?.[current],own=(stage?.fixtures||[]).find(f=>f?.home===world.user||f?.away===world.user);
    if(!stage||!own)throw Error('A partida do seu clube nao foi encontrada na jornada atual.');
    if(live.fixtureId!==own.id)throw Error('A partida finalizada nao corresponde ao jogo do seu clube nesta jornada.');
        if(o.team&&Array.isArray(world.teams)){
      const ix=world.teams.findIndex(t=>t?.id===o.team.id);
      if(ix>=0){
        const resident=world.teams[ix],incoming=o.team;
        const rStats=resident?.stats,iStats=incoming?.stats;
        const stats=rStats&&(Number(rStats.p)||0)>=(Number(iStats?.p)||0)?rStats:iStats;
        world.teams[ix]={...resident,...incoming,stats:stats||incoming.stats};
      }
    }
    world.live=live;v157HumanOverlay=true;
    if(o.companions&&typeof o.companions==='object')world.liveCompanions=o.companions;else delete world.liveCompanions;
    if(world.vacation){world.vacation.active=false;world.vacation.paused=true;world.vacation.reason='';}
   }
  }
  // 15.2: o periodo escolhido na interface e autoritativo. O snapshot em disco
  // pode conter as ferias anteriores ja encerradas; nunca reutilize remaining=0.
  if(message.mode==='vacation'&&message.vacationState){
   world.vacation={...(world.vacation||{}),...message.vacationState,active:true,paused:false};
  }
  const lowMemoryVacation=message.mode==='vacation',lowMemoryDay=message.mode==='day';
  // Ferias permanece exatamente no fluxo de baixa memoria da v48.
  // O modo dayfast usa o mesmo principio de baixo overhead, mas processa UMA unica jornada
  // e sincroniza o mundo sem destruir a copia residente do Worker.
  if(lowMemoryVacation||lowMemoryDay){baseline=null;checkpoint=null;v126InitLive();}
  else if(fastManualDay||nationalJump||nextMatchJump){baseline=null;checkpoint=null;if(nationalJump||nextMatchJump)v126InitLive();}
  else{baseline=baselineOf(world);checkpoint=await encode(world);}
  self.postMessage({kind:'started',session});
  let failure=null,streamed=false,finalDay=world?.day,finalYear=world?.year,deferredFinalSave=null;
  try{
   if(message.mode==='finish'){
    if(!world.live)throw Error('Partida ausente.');E.finishMatch(world,world.live);const saved=await save(world);publish(true);self.postMessage({kind:'saved',session,...saved});
   }else if(fastManualDay){
    if(pause)applyPause();
    if(!v157AlreadyConsolidated){
     // Reinstall the captured final match immediately before the engine call. This
     // makes the guard in advanceDayAsync deterministic even if another save/update
     // touched the resident world while the request was queued.
     if(message.manualOverlay&&v157HumanOverlay){
      world.live=message.manualOverlay.live;
      if(message.manualOverlay.companions&&typeof message.manualOverlay.companions==='object')world.liveCompanions=message.manualOverlay.companions;else delete world.liveCompanions;
     }
     const nationalToken=National.before(world);
     if(nationalToken?.userPending)throw Error('Conclua a partida da sua selecao antes de avancar.');
     const oldFastManual=world.v158FastManualEngine;
     world.v158FastManualEngine=true;
     try{
      // v60: o motor recebe a partida finalizada diretamente. Nao existe mais
      // tentativa baseada em world.live nem retry do antigo guard pos-jogo.
      const postMatchOptions=message.manualOverlay?{
       humanFinal:message.manualOverlay.live,
       humanCompanions:message.manualOverlay.companions||null
      }:null;
      await E.advanceDayAsync(world,progress,postMatchOptions);
     }finally{
      if(oldFastManual===undefined)delete world.v158FastManualEngine;else world.v158FastManualEngine=oldFastManual;
     }
     National.after(world,nationalToken);
     applyPause();
     if(world.seasonDone&&world.vacation?.untilSeason){world.vacation.remaining=0;world.vacation.untilSeason=false;world.vacation.active=false;world.vacation.paused=true;}
    }
    finalDay=world.day;finalYear=world.year;
    // One manual click uses the same light checkpoint as vacation. For a finished
    // human match an explicit close marker is also sent; the UI no longer depends on
    // a generic root delta to discover that the live session ended.
    await v126PublishVacationLive(false);
    if(message.manualOverlay)self.postMessage({kind:'postmatch-closed',session,day:world.day,lastDay:world.lastDay,sourceDay:v157HumanDay});
    streamed=true;
   }else if(lowMemoryDay){
    if(pause)applyPause();const nationalToken=National.before(world);if(nationalToken?.userPending)throw Error('Conclua a partida da sua selecao antes de avancar.');

    finalDay=world.day;finalYear=world.year;await v126PublishVacationLive(false);streamed=true;
   }else if(nationalJump){
    // 13.4: advance the club calendar entirely in the Worker and stop BEFORE
    // the next unresolved match of the user's national team. This avoids the
    // old main-thread while-loop used by "Avancar ate a selecao".
    if(pause)applyPause();

    const ownNationalCode=String(message.nationalContext?.code||'').toUpperCase();

     const stage=world.schedule?.[world.day];
     const lastNationalStage=stage?.type==='national'&&!(world.schedule||[]).some((d,i)=>i>Number(world.day)&&d?.type==='national');
     const nationalToken=National.before(world);
     if(nationalToken?.userPending){stoppedForNational=true;break;}
     if(stage?.type==='national'&&stage.v640Kind==='window'){
      const games=nationalToken?.stage?.nationalFixtures||[];

      if(!games.some(f=>f.home===ownNationalCode||f.away===ownNationalCode)){
       // Uma folga e permitida quando a confederacao tem numero impar de selecoes.
       // Duas janelas seguidas sem compromisso indicam calendario incoerente.
       if(++missingUserWindowDates>=2)throw Error('O calendario da selecao nao criou jogos em duas Datas FIFA consecutivas. Avanco interrompido.');
      }else missingUserWindowDates=0;
     }

     await v126PublishVacationLive(false);

     // Se a selecao nao se classificou para os ultimos mata-matas, encerra o
     // salto logo apos a ultima Data FIFA, sem consumir o restante da temporada.
     if(lastNationalStage){stoppedForNational=true;break;}
     await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    if(!stoppedForNational){
     // No valid user game was found: never pretend that finishing the season
     // means the requested international appointment was reached.
     throw Error(world.seasonDone?'Nenhum compromisso da sua selecao foi encontrado antes do fim da temporada. Verifique o calendario FIFA.':'Limite de jornadas atingido sem encontrar a proxima partida da selecao.');
    }
    // Synchronize only the national-team data that can matter immediately for
    // call-up decisions instead of retransmitting the entire player database.
    const code=world?.v640National?.userTeam;

     const nt=world.v640National?.teams?.[code]||null,rows=[];
     const candidates=Object.values(world.players||{}).filter(p=>p&&String(p.natCode||'').toUpperCase()===String(code).toUpperCase()&&Number(p.age)>=16).sort((a,b)=>E.overall(b)-E.overall(a)||(Number(b.fitness)||0)-(Number(a.fitness)||0)).slice(0,120);
     const ids=new Set([...(nt?.roster||[]),...candidates.map(p=>p.id)]);
     for(const id of ids){const p=world.players?.[id];if(p)rows.push([id,p]);}
     const currentStage=world.schedule?.[world.day]||null,competitionStatRows=[];
     // 13.8: o salto internacional precisa devolver tambem a producao ofensiva dos clubes.
     // Envia apenas linhas com gol/assistencia, suficiente para fichas, artilharia e carreira atual,
     // sem retransmitir todo o banco de jogadores.
     for(const [key,db] of Object.entries(world.competitionStats||{}))for(const [pid,r] of Object.entries(db||{})){
      if(!r||((Number(r.goals)||0)<=0&&(Number(r.assists)||0)<=0))continue;
      competitionStatRows.push([key,pid,{id:r.id||pid,name:r.name||'',natCode:r.natCode||'UNK',goals:Number(r.goals)||0,assists:Number(r.assists)||0,apps:Number(r.apps)||0,minutes:Number(r.minutes)||0,clubs:r.clubs||{},byClub:r.byClub||{}}]);
     }
     self.postMessage({kind:'national-jump-sync',session,code,nationalTeam:nt,playerRows:rows,competitionStatRows,stageIndex:Number(world.day)||0,stage:currentStage,day:world.day,year:world.year,stoppedForNational});
    }
    streamed=true;
   }

function v533RepairCareer(s){if(!s?.players)return 0;let changed=0;const owner=new Map();for(const t of s.teams||[]){for(const id of t.roster||[])owner.set(id,t);for(const id of t.academy||[])owner.set(id,t);for(const id of t.proReserve||[])owner.set(id,t);}

 return changed;}

function v536SeasonGA(id){let goals=0,assists=0,found=false;for(const rows of Object.values(state?.competitionStats||{})){const r=rows?.[id];if(!r)continue;found=true;goals+=Number(r.goals)||0;assists+=Number(r.assists)||0;}if(!found){const p=state?E.player(state,id):null;goals=Number(p?.goals)||0;assists=Number(p?.assists)||0;}return {goals,assists};}

function v538PaintTile(html,target){if(!html)return html;const t=v538KitTeam(target);if(!t)return html;const vars=v538KitVarsForTeam(t);html=html.replace(/class="(v5-(?:reserve|footballer))\s/,`class="$1 v538-kit-card `);const buttonEnd=html.indexOf('>');if(buttonEnd<0)return html;const head=html.slice(0,buttonEnd),tail=html.slice(buttonEnd);if(/\sstyle="/.test(head))return head.replace(/style="([^"]*)"/,(_,s)=>`style="${vars}${s}"`)+tail;return head+` style="${vars}"`+tail;}
const v538BaseTile=v5Tile;

function v538ReadonlyTile(p,target,slot,live,team,bench=false,used=false){const role=bench?v5Role(p):E.slotRole(target,slot),fitv=bench?1:E.positionFit(p,role),xy=bench?null:E.slotCoords(target,slot),energy=Math.round(target.energy?.[p.id]??p.fitness),rating=!bench&&live?v57PlayerRating(live.m,live.index,p.id):null,warn=!bench&&fitv<.93?`<span class="v531-outwarn ${v5FitClass(fitv)}">${fitv<.78?'Fora da posição':'Adaptado'}</span>`:'',vars=v538KitVarsForTeam(team);return `<button type="button" class="${bench?'v5-reserve':'v5-footballer'} v538-kit-card ${v525PosClass(p)} ${bench&&used?'unavailable':''} ${v5FitClass(fitv)}" ${bench?'':`style="${vars}left:${xy[0]}%;top:${xy[1]}%"`} ${bench?`style="${vars}"`:''} data-action="v513-player-view" data-player="${esc(p.id)}" title="${esc(p.name)} · ${esc(role)} · OVR ${E.overall(p)}"><span class="v5-shirt ${p.pos==='GOL'?'goalkeeper':''}">${E.overall(p)}</span><span class="v5-tile-name">${esc(p.name)}</span><span class="v5-tile-meta">${esc(v5Role(p))} · OVR ${E.overall(p)}</span><span class="v5-energy"><i style="width:${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,energy))}%"></i></span>${warn}${rating==null?'':`<span class="v57-live-rating ${v57RatingClass(rating)}">${rating.toFixed(1)}</span>`}</button>`;}
v537ReadonlyPitch=function(teamId){const t=E.team(state,teamId),live=v513SideForTeam(teamId),target=live?.side||{tactics:t.tactics,lineup:t.lineup,energy:Object.fromEntries(t.roster.map(id=>[id,E.player(state,id).fitness]))};return `<div class="v5-pitch pitch" aria-label="Prancheta de análise ${esc(t.name)}">${v526ZoneLayer()}<div class="pitch-circle"></div><div class="pitch-box top"></div><div class="pitch-box bottom"></div><span class="pitch-direction">ATAQUE &uarr;</span><span class="pitch-watermark">${esc(target.tactics.formation)} &middot; SOMENTE LEITURA</span>${(target.lineup||[]).map((id,slot)=>{const p=id&&E.player(state,id);return p?v538ReadonlyTile(p,target,slot,live,t,false,false):'';}).join('')}</div>`;};
v537ReadonlyBench=function(teamId){const t=E.team(state,teamId),live=v513SideForTeam(teamId),side=live?.side,target=side||{lineup:t.lineup,bench:t.roster.filter(id=>!t.lineup.includes(id)),used:[],reds:[],energy:Object.fromEntries(t.roster.map(id=>[id,E.player(state,id).fitness]))},ids=target.bench||[];return `<div class="v5-bench v525-grouped-bench">${V525_POSITIONS.map(pos=>{const group=ids.filter(id=>E.player(state,id)?.pos===pos);if(!group.length)return '';return `<section class="v525-bench-group v525-pos-${pos}"><div class="v525-bench-head">${V525_POS_LABEL[pos]}<span>${group.length}</span></div><div class="v525-bench-grid">${group.map(id=>{const p=E.player(state,id),used=target.used?.includes(id);return v538ReadonlyTile(p,target,null,live,t,true,used);}).join('')}</div></section>`;}).join('')}</div>`;};

function v538CompName(key){const parts=String(key||'').split(':'),country=C.find(c=>c.id===parts[1]);if(parts[0]==='league')return `${country?.name||parts[1]||'Liga'} · ${parts[2]||'1'}ª divisão`;if(parts[0]==='cup')return country?.cup||`Copa · ${country?.name||parts[1]||''}`;return state?.competitions?.[key]?.name||V51_CUPS?.[key]||E.competitionLabel(state,key)||String(key||'Competição').toUpperCase();}
function v538CareerRow(map,year,teamId,teamName){const key=`${year}|${teamId||teamName||'unknown'}`;if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{year:Number(year),teamId:teamId||null,teamName:teamName||E.team(state,teamId)?.name||'Clube não identificado',goals:0,assists:0,yellow:0,red:0,legacy:false,clubIds:teamId?[teamId]:[]});return map.get(key);}
function v538CurrentSeasonRows(pid){const p=E.player(state,pid);if(!p)return [];const map=new Map();for(const rows of Object.values(state.competitionStats||{})){const r=rows?.[pid];if(!r)continue;for(const [tid,name] of Object.entries(r.clubs||{}))v538CareerRow(map,state.year,tid,name);}
 for(const stage of state.schedule||[])for(const f of stage.fixtures||[]){const r=f.result;if(!r?.events)continue;for(const e of r.events){const tid=e.side===0?f.home:e.side===1?f.away:null;if(!tid)continue;const t=E.team(state,tid),row=v538CareerRow(map,state.year,tid,t?.name);if(e.pid===pid){if(e.type==='goal')row.goals++;if(e.type==='yellow')row.yellow++;if(e.type==='red')row.red++;}if(e.type==='goal'&&e.assistName&&E.normName(e.assistName)===E.normName(p.name))row.assists++;}}
 const totals=v536SeasonGA(pid),rows=[...map.values()],sum=(k)=>rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r[k])||0),0),owner=E.owner(state,pid),fallback=owner?v538CareerRow(map,state.year,owner.id,owner.name):(rows[0]||null);if(fallback){fallback.goals+=Math.max(0,(totals.goals||0)-sum('goals'));fallback.assists+=Math.max(0,(totals.assists||0)-sum('assists'));fallback.yellow+=Math.max(0,(p.yellows||0)-sum('yellow'));fallback.red+=Math.max(0,(p.reds||0)-sum('red'));}return [...map.values()];}
function v538ArchiveSeason(s){if(!s||!s.seasonDone)return false;s.v538CareerArchive??={};if(s.v538CareerArchive[s.year])return false;const season={};for(const p of Object.values(s.players||{})){const rows=v538CurrentSeasonRows(p.id).filter(r=>r.goals||r.assists||r.yellow||r.red);if(rows.length)season[p.id]=rows.map(r=>({year:r.year,teamId:r.teamId,teamName:r.teamName,goals:r.goals,assists:r.assists,yellow:r.yellow,red:r.red}));}s.v538CareerArchive[s.year]=season;return true;}
function v538LegacyRows(pid){const out=[];for(const a of state.scorerArchive||[]){if(state.v538CareerArchive?.[a.year])continue;let goals=0,assists=0,clubs={};for(const rows of Object.values(a.competitions||{})){const r=rows?.[pid];if(!r)continue;goals+=Number(r.goals)||0;assists+=Number(r.assists)||0;Object.assign(clubs,r.clubs||{});}if(!goals&&!assists)continue;const ids=Object.keys(clubs);if(ids.length===1)out.push({year:a.year,teamId:ids[0],teamName:clubs[ids[0]],goals,assists,yellow:null,red:null,legacy:true,clubIds:ids});else out.push({year:a.year,teamId:null,teamName:ids.length?Object.values(clubs).join(' / '):'Clube não registrado',goals,assists,yellow:null,red:null,legacy:true,clubIds:ids});}return out;}
function v538CareerRows(pid){const rows=[];for(const [year,season] of Object.entries(state.v538CareerArchive||{}))for(const r of season?.[pid]||[])rows.push({...r,year:Number(year),legacy:false,clubIds:r.teamId?[r.teamId]:[]});if(!state.v538CareerArchive?.[state.year])rows.push(...v538CurrentSeasonRows(pid));rows.push(...v538LegacyRows(pid));return rows.sort((a,b)=>b.year-a.year||(a.teamName||'').localeCompare(b.teamName||'','pt-BR'));}
function v538ClubIdentity(teamId,name='Clube não registrado'){const t=teamId?E.team(state,teamId):null;if(!t)return `<div class="v538-legacy-clubs">${esc(name)}</div>`;return `<div class="v538-club-cell"><div class="v538-club-main">${badge(t,'small')}${teamButton(t)}</div><div class="v538-country-mini">${v532FlagHTML(v530CountryNat(t.country))}<span>${esc(countryName(t.country))}</span></div></div>`;}
function v538CareerClubCell(r){const ids=[...new Set((r.clubIds||[]).filter(Boolean))];if(r.teamId&&!ids.includes(r.teamId))ids.unshift(r.teamId);if(!ids.length)return v538ClubIdentity(r.teamId,r.teamName);return `<div class="v538-career-clubs">${ids.map(id=>v538ClubIdentity(id,E.team(state,id)?.name||r.teamName)).join('')}</div>`;}
function v538CareerHTML(pid){const rows=v538CareerRows(pid),tot=rows.reduce((a,r)=>({g:a.g+(r.goals||0),a:a.a+(r.assists||0),y:a.y+(r.yellow||0),r:a.r+(r.red||0)}),{g:0,a:0,y:0,r:0}),legacy=rows.some(r=>r.legacy);return `<div class="v538-player-summary"><div><strong>${tot.g}</strong><span>Gols registrados</span></div><div><strong>${tot.a}</strong><span>Assistências</span></div><div><strong>${tot.y}</strong><span>Amarelos arquivados</span></div><div><strong>${tot.r}</strong><span>Vermelhos arquivados</span></div></div>${legacy?'<p class="v538-career-note">Temporadas anteriores à ficha histórica 5.38 preservam gols e assistências do arquivo de artilharia. Cartões antigos que não eram arquivados por temporada aparecem como “—”.</p>':''}${rows.length?`<div class="table-scroll"><table class="v538-career-table"><thead><tr><th>Ano</th><th class="v538-career-club">Clube / país</th><th class="number">Gols</th><th class="number">Assist.</th><th class="number">AM</th><th class="number">VM</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr><td><strong>${r.year}</strong></td><td>${v538CareerClubCell(r)}</td><td class="number green strong">${r.goals||0}</td><td class="number">${r.assists||0}</td><td class="number">${r.yellow==null?'—':r.yellow}</td><td class="number">${r.red==null?'—':r.red}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="empty">Este atleta ainda não possui produção registrada em uma temporada da carreira.</div>'}`;}

function v538AddHonor(out,h){const key=[h.kind,h.year,h.key,h.teamId||h.teamName||''].join('|');if(!out.has(key))out.set(key,h);}
function v538HistoricalHonors(pid){const out=new Map(),p=E.player(state,pid);if(!p)return [];
 for(const h of p.honors538||[])v538AddHonor(out,h);
 const participation=new Map();for(const r of v538CareerRows(pid)){const tids=r.clubIds?.length?r.clubIds:(r.teamId?[r.teamId]:[]);for(const tid of tids){if(!tid)continue;const k=`${r.year}|${tid}`;participation.set(k,true);}}
 for(const [k] of participation){const [yearS,tid]=k.split('|'),year=Number(yearS);for(const t of v59ClubHonors(tid).filter(x=>Number(x.year)===year))v538AddHonor(out,{kind:'club',year,key:t.key,name:t.name,teamId:tid,teamName:E.team(state,tid)?.name||t.clubName});}
 for(const a of state.scorerArchive||[]){for(const [key,db] of Object.entries(a.competitions||{})){const rows=Object.values(db||{});if(!rows.length)continue;const max=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>Number(r.goals)||0));if(max<=0)continue;const r=db?.[pid];if(!r||Number(r.goals)!==max)continue;const ids=Object.keys(r.clubs||{}),tid=ids.at(-1)||null;v538AddHonor(out,{kind:'scorer',year:a.year,key:'scorer:'+key,name:'Artilheiro · '+v538CompName(key),teamId:tid,teamName:tid?E.team(state,tid)?.name:(Object.values(r.clubs||{}).at(-1)||'Clube não registrado'),goals:max});}}
 return [...out.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.year-a.year||(a.kind==='scorer'?-1:1));}
function v538HonorsHTML(pid){const rows=v538HistoricalHonors(pid);return rows.length?`<div class="v538-honor-grid">${rows.map(h=>`<article class="v538-honor-card ${h.kind==='scorer'?'scorer':''}"><div class="v538-honor-top"><div><span class="v538-honor-type">${h.kind==='scorer'?icon('ball',12)+' Artilharia':icon('trophy',12)+' Título do clube'}</span><h4>${esc(h.name)}</h4></div><span class="v538-honor-year">${h.year}</span></div>${h.goals?`<p class="tiny gold mb8">${h.goals} gol(s) na competição</p>`:''}${v538ClubIdentity(h.teamId,h.teamName)}</article>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty"><h3>Sem conquistas registradas</h3><p>Títulos do clube e artilharias conquistados durante a carreira aparecerão aqui.</p></div>';}

function v538AwardSeasonHonors(s){if(!s?.seasonDone)return false;s.v538HonorsYears??={};if(s.v538HonorsYears[s.year])return false;const year=s.year,participants=new Map();
 const addParticipant=(tid,pid)=>{if(!tid||!pid||!s.players?.[pid])return;if(!participants.has(tid))participants.set(tid,new Set());participants.get(tid).add(pid);};
 for(const [key,db] of Object.entries(s.competitionStats||{}))for(const r of Object.values(db||{}))for(const tid of Object.keys(r?.clubs||{}))addParticipant(tid,r.id);
 for(const t of s.teams||[])for(const pid of t.roster||[])addParticipant(t.id,pid);
 for(const t of s.teams||[]){const honors=v59ClubHonors(t.id).filter(h=>Number(h.year)===Number(year));if(!honors.length)continue;for(const pid of participants.get(t.id)||[]){const p=s.players[pid];p.honors538??=[];for(const h of honors){const item={kind:'club',year,key:h.key,name:h.name,teamId:t.id,teamName:t.name};const sig=[item.kind,item.year,item.key,item.teamId].join('|');if(!p.honors538.some(x=>[x.kind,x.year,x.key,x.teamId].join('|')===sig))p.honors538.push(item);}}}
 for(const [key,db] of Object.entries(s.competitionStats||{})){const rows=Object.values(db||{});if(!rows.length)continue;const max=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>Number(r.goals)||0));if(max<=0)continue;for(const r of rows.filter(x=>(Number(x.goals)||0)===max)){const p=s.players?.[r.id];if(!p)continue;const tids=Object.keys(r.clubs||{}),tid=tids.at(-1)||p.clubId||null,team=tid?E.team(s,tid):null,item={kind:'scorer',year,key:'scorer:'+key,name:'Artilheiro · '+v538CompName(key),teamId:tid,teamName:team?.name||Object.values(r.clubs||{}).at(-1)||'Clube não registrado',goals:max};p.honors538??=[];const sig=[item.kind,item.year,item.key,item.teamId].join('|');if(!p.honors538.some(x=>[x.kind,x.year,x.key,x.teamId].join('|')===sig))p.honors538.push(item);}}
 s.v538HonorsYears[year]=true;return true;}
const v538BaseNextSeason=E.nextSeason;
E.nextSeason=function(s){if(s?.seasonDone){v538AwardSeasonHonors(s);v538ArchiveSeason(s);}return v538BaseNextSeason(s);};

function v602TacticalPitchTile(html,p,target,slot){

 const selected=v602SortPlayerIds(v600NormalizePreferredBench(state,t)),all=v602SortPlayerIds(v600BenchCandidates(state,t)),outside=all.filter(id=>!selected.includes(id));
 t.v600PreferredBench=[...selected];
 const outOptions=selected.length?selected.map(id=>`<option value="${id}">${esc(v600BenchOption(id))}</option>`).join(''):'<option value="">Sem jogador</option>';
 const inOptions=outside.length?outside.map(id=>`<option value="${id}">${esc(v600BenchOption(id))}</option>`).join(''):'<option value="">Sem alternativas</option>';
 return `<div class="v600-bench-manager"><div class="v600-bench-swap"><div class="field"><label for="v600-bench-out">Sai do banco</label><select id="v600-bench-out" ${!selected.length?'disabled':''}>${outOptions}</select></div><div class="field"><label for="v600-bench-in">Entra no banco</label><select id="v600-bench-in" ${!outside.length?'disabled':''}>${inOptions}</select></div>${button('v600-bench-swap','Trocar','small secondary',!selected.length||!outside.length?'disabled':'')}${button('v600-bench-auto','Auto','small ghost')}</div><div class="v600-bench-list" data-v5-bench="club">${selected.map(id=>v5Tile(id,t,'club')).join('')}</div></div>`;
};

const v602BaseExecute=v5Execute;
v5Execute=function(source,destination,x,y){
 const ctx=source?.ctx;
 if(ctx==='club'&&state&&!state.userEmployed)throw Error('Assuma um clube em Treinadores para voltar a gerenciar.');
 if(view==='tactics'&&['club','national'].includes(ctx)&&destination?.dataset?.v5Player&&destination.dataset.v5Context===ctx){
  const t=ctx==='national'?v133EnsureNationalReferenceRoster():E.human(state),sid=source.id,did=destination.dataset.v5Player;
  const sOn=(t?.lineup||[]).includes(sid),dOn=(t?.lineup||[]).includes(did);
  if(t&&!sOn&&!dOn&&sid!==did){
   const pref=[...(ctx==='national'?v133NormalizeNationalBench(t):v600NormalizePreferredBench(state,t))],si=pref.indexOf(sid),di=pref.indexOf(did);
   if((si>=0)!==(di>=0)){
    const idx=si>=0?si:di,inc=si>=0?did:sid;
    pref[idx]=inc;t.v600BenchManual=true;t.v600PreferredBench=v602SortPlayerIds([...new Set(pref)]).slice(0,V600_BENCH_LIMIT);
    v5FinishEdit(ctx);toast('Relação do banco atualizada.');return;
   }
  }
 }
 return v602BaseExecute(source,destination,x,y);
};

  });

const V603_VERSION='6.03';
const V603_POS_ORDER={GOL:0,ZAG:1,LAT:2,VOL:3,MEI:4,ATA:5};
const V603_POS_LABEL={GOL:'Goleiro',ZAG:'Zagueiro',LAT:'Lateral',VOL:'Volante',MEI:'Meia',ATA:'Atacante'};
function v603AcademyPlayers(t){
 return (t?.academy||[]).map(id=>E.player(state,id)).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>(V603_POS_ORDER[a.pos]??99)-(V603_POS_ORDER[b.pos]??99)||E.overall(b)-E.overall(a)||(b.potential||0)-(a.potential||0)||String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'pt-BR'));
}
function v603YouthEvo(p){const gain=E.overall(p)-(p.youthStart??E.overall(p));return `<span class="v58-evo ${gain>0?'up':gain<0?'down':''}">${gain>0?'+':''}${gain||'—'}</span>`;}
function v603AcademyRows(t,own=false){
 const ps=v603AcademyPlayers(t);
 return ps.map(p=>`<tr class="v603-pos-${p.pos}"><td><button class="table-button player-name" data-action="v4-youth-profile" data-player="${p.id}">${esc(p.name)}</button>${typeof v523FlagImg==='function'?v523FlagImg(p.natCode||'BRA','v523-player-flag'):playerMeta(p)}</td><td><span class="role v523-pos-${p.pos}">${V603_POS_LABEL[p.pos]||p.pos}</span></td><td>${p.age}${p.ageEstimated?'*':''}</td><td>${rating(E.overall(p))}</td><td class="gold strong">${p.potential}</td><td>${v603YouthEvo(p)}</td><td class="number">${p.academyApps||0}</td><td>${own?button('v4-promote','Promover','small',`data-player="${p.id}" ${p.age<16?'disabled':''}`):button('v4-youth-profile','Perfil','small ghost',`data-player="${p.id}"`)}</td></tr>`).join('');
}

 v4AcademyClub=t.id;
 return `<div class="v603-academy-page"><section class="panel v603-academy-panel"><div class="panel-head v603-academy-head"><div class="v603-academy-title">${badge(t,'small')}<div><h2>Categorias de Base</h2><div class="v603-academy-summary"><span class="pill success">${ps.length} JOVENS</span><span>Centro nível ${t.youthLevel||1}</span><span>OVR médio ${avg||'—'}</span><span>Pot. máx. ${maxPot||'—'}</span></div></div></div><div class="v603-academy-plan"><label for="academy-plan">Plano de formação</label><select id="academy-plan" data-v4="academy-plan" ${state.vacation.active||state.live?'disabled':''}><option value="balanced" ${t.academyPlan!=='intensive'?'selected':''}>Equilibrado</option><option value="intensive" ${t.academyPlan==='intensive'?'selected':''}>Intensivo</option></select></div></div><div class="v603-academy-table-wrap"><table class="v57-squad-table v58-squad-table v603-academy-table"><thead><tr><th>Atleta</th><th>Posição</th><th>Idade</th><th>OVR</th><th>Pot.</th><th>Evol.</th><th class="number">Jogos</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${v603AcademyRows(t,true)}</tbody></table></div><div class="v603-academy-foot"><span>Idade máxima: 20 anos · promoção disponível a partir dos 16</span><span>GOL → ZAG → LAT → VOL → MEI → ATA</span></div></section></div>`;

FUNCAO COM ESTATISTICAS: v658StateStamp @ 12260740
##########################################################################################
function v658StateStamp(s){
 if(!s)return 'none';
 const comps=Object.values(s.competitions||{}),champions=comps.reduce((n,c)=>n+(c?.champion?1:0),0);
 return [Number(s.year)||0,Number(s.day)||0,s.seasonDone?1:0,Object.keys(s.cupWinners||{}).length,champions,(s.internationalHistory||[]).length,(s.v652ClubPerformanceHistory||[]).length,(s.v657ClubRecordHistory||[]).length,(s.scorerArchive||[]).length,Object.keys(s.coaches||{}).length].join('|');
}

##########################################################################################
FUNCAO COM ESTATISTICAS: v690FootyExactAssets @ 12660688
