function v690FootyExactAssets(slug,task){try{const txt=await v690FetchText('https://r.jina.ai/http://www.footylogos.com/logos/'+slug,task,10000),out=[],re=/https:\/\/assets\.footylogos\.com\/[^\s\)\]"']+?\.(?:png|svg)(?:\?[^\s\)\]"']*)?/gi;let m;while((m=re.exec(txt)))if(!out.includes(m[0]))out.push(m[0]);return out;}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;return [];}}
async function v690FootyResolve(t,task){const m=await v690FootyMatch(t,task),urls=[...v690FootyAssetGuesses(m.slug),...(await v690FootyExactAssets(m.slug,task))];for(const url of [...new Set(urls)]){const c={url,provider:'FootyLogos · país confirmado',source:'https://www.footylogos.com/logos/'+m.slug,identity:`${m.name} · ${countryName(t.country)} · página de país + nome confirmados`,clubName:t.name,country:t.country,verified:true,score:m.score};if(await v688SaveSmart(t,c,task))return true;}await v687KeepReview(t,{url:urls[0],provider:'FootyLogos · país confirmado',source:'https://www.footylogos.com/logos/'+m.slug,identity:`${m.name} · país confirmado; imagem não incorporada`,clubName:t.name,country:t.country,verified:false});return false;}

function v690FotmobTeams(raw){const arr=[];for(const x of (raw?.team||raw?.teams||[])){if(x?.id&&x?.name)arr.push({id:x.id,name:x.localizedName||x.name,raw:x});}if(Array.isArray(raw?.suggestions))for(const x of raw.suggestions){if(String(x?.type||'').toLowerCase()==='team'&&x?.id&&x?.name)arr.push({id:x.id,name:x.name,raw:x});}if(Array.isArray(raw?.groups))for(const g of raw.groups)for(const x of (g?.suggestions||[]))if(String(x?.type||'').toLowerCase()==='team'&&x?.id&&x?.name)arr.push({id:x.id,name:x.name,raw:x});return [...new Map(arr.map(x=>[String(x.id),x])).values()];}
async function v690FotmobSearch(t,task){let rows=[];for(const term of v690NamePool(t).slice(0,2)){try{let raw=await v690FetchJSON('https://www.fotmob.com/api/searchData?term='+encodeURIComponent(term),task);rows.push(...v690FotmobTeams(raw));if(rows.length)break;}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}}rows=[...new Map(rows.map(x=>[String(x.id),x])).values()].map(x=>({...x,score:v690Score(t,x.name)})).filter(x=>x.score>=92).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,5);if(!rows.length)throw Error('FotMob: nenhum clube compatível.');for(const row of rows){try{const detail=await v690FetchJSON(`https://www.fotmob.com/api/data/teams?id=${encodeURIComponent(row.id)}&ccode3=${encodeURIComponent(String(t.country).toUpperCase())}`,task),d=detail?.details||detail?.team||detail;if(!v690CountryEvidence(t,d))continue;const name=d?.name||row.name,score=Math.max(row.score,v690Score(t,name));if(score<104)continue;v690ApiCanonical.set(t.id,name);return {url:`https://images.fotmob.com/image_resources/logo/teamlogo/${row.id}.png`,provider:'FotMob · ID + país confirmados',source:`https://www.fotmob.com/teams/${row.id}/overview`,identity:`${name} · ID ${row.id} · país ${String(t.country).toUpperCase()} confirmado pela ficha da equipe`,clubName:t.name,country:t.country,verified:true,score};}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}}throw Error('FotMob: homônimo ou país não confirmado.');}

function v690SofaRows(raw){const out=[];for(const r of (raw?.results||[])){const e=r?.entity||r;if(!e?.id||!e?.name)continue;const type=String(r?.type||e?.entityType||'').toLowerCase(),sport=e?.sport?.id||e?.sport?.slug;if(type&&type!=='team'&&type!=='teams')continue;if(!type&&!(sport===1||sport==='football')&&!e.shortName)continue;out.push({id:e.id,name:e.name,entity:e,raw:r});}return [...new Map(out.map(x=>[String(x.id),x])).values()];}
async function v690SofaSearch(t,task){let rows=[];for(const term of v690NamePool(t).slice(0,2)){try{const raw=await v690FetchJSON('https://api.sofascore.com/api/v1/search/all?q='+encodeURIComponent(term),task);rows.push(...v690SofaRows(raw));if(rows.length)break;}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}}rows=[...new Map(rows.map(x=>[String(x.id),x])).values()].map(x=>({...x,score:v690Score(t,x.name)})).filter(x=>x.score>=92).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,6);if(!rows.length)throw Error('SofaScore: nenhum clube compatível.');for(const row of rows){try{const raw=await v690FetchJSON('https://api.sofascore.com/api/v1/team/'+encodeURIComponent(row.id),task),team=raw?.team||raw;if(!team||!v690CountryEvidence(t,team))continue;const name=team.name||row.name,score=Math.max(row.score,v690Score(t,name));if(score<104)continue;v690ApiCanonical.set(t.id,name);return {url:`https://img.sofascore.com/api/v1/team/${row.id}/image`,provider:'SofaScore · ID + país confirmados',source:`https://www.sofascore.com/team/football/${team.slug||'club'}/${row.id}`,identity:`${name} · ID ${row.id} · país confirmado pela ficha da equipe`,clubName:t.name,country:t.country,verified:true,score};}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}}throw Error('SofaScore: homônimo ou país não confirmado.');}

async function v690TryApiCandidate(t,resolver,task){try{const c=await resolver(t,task);if(c?.verified&&await v688SaveSmart(t,c,task))return true;if(c)await v687KeepReview(t,c);}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}return false;}
async function v690CanonicalWiki(t,task){const hint=v690FootyCanonical.get(t.id)||v690ApiCanonical.get(t.id)||(V690_ALIAS_HINTS[t.id]||[])[0];if(!hint)return false;try{const syn=v690Synthetic(t),c=await v688WikipediaLinkedCrest(syn,task);if(await v688TryCandidates(t,[c],task))return true;}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;}return false;}
function v690Finalize(t,kind){if(kind==='safe')v686CrestRun.safe690++;if(kind==='footy')v686CrestRun.footy690++;if(kind==='sofa')v686CrestRun.sofa690++;if(kind==='fotmob')v686CrestRun.fotmob690++;if(kind==='wiki')v686CrestRun.wiki690++;v687Finalize(t,'search');}

v686CrestRunText=function(){const a=v686CrestAudit(),r=v686CrestRun;if(r.active)return `${r.phase||'Pronto'}: ${r.phaseDone}/${r.phaseTotal} · total ${r.done}/${r.total} · ${r.safe690||0} curados · ${r.footy690||0} FootyLogos · ${r.sofa690||0} SofaScore · ${r.fotmob690||0} FotMob · ${r.wiki690||0} Wiki final · ${r.review} revisão · ${r.failed} falhas${r.lastClub?' · '+r.lastClub:''}`;return `${a.local}/${a.total} clubes com escudo local · ${a.missing} pendentes · ${a.review} em revisão`;};

function v691ApplyCatalogMap(map){
 if(!map||typeof map!=='object')return 0;let n=0;
 for(const [id,raw] of Object.entries(map)){
  if(!raw||!C.some(c=>c.id===raw.country))continue;
  const rec={...raw,id,aliases:[...new Set([raw.name,...(raw.aliases||[])].filter(Boolean))]};
  let t=WorldCatalog.clubs.find(x=>x.id===id);
  if(t)Object.assign(t,rec);else{t=rec;WorldCatalog.clubs.push(t);n++;}
  v4Catalog.set(id,t);
 }
 v691RefreshMeta();return n;
}

function v691Hash(s){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(const ch of String(s||'')){h^=ch.codePointAt(0);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0;}return h>>>0;}
function v691StableId(country,name){const core=v688Compact(name).replace(/\s+/g,'-').slice(0,34)||'club',h=v691Hash(country+'|'+v688Fold(name)).toString(36);return `v691_${country}_${core}_${h}`;}
function v691Short(name){const words=String(name||'').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]/gu,' ').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);let s=words.length>1?words.slice(0,4).map(x=>x[0]).join(''):String(name||'').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu,'').slice(0,4);return s.toUpperCase().slice(0,5)||'FC';}
function v691Color(name){const h=v691Hash(name)%360;return `hsl(${h} 34% 42%)`;}
function v691CountryAliasIndex(){
 const m=new Map(),put=(raw,id)=>{const k=v688Fold(raw);if(!k)return;if(!m.has(k))m.set(k,new Set());m.get(k).add(id);};
 for(const c of C){put(c.name,c.id);put(c.id,c.id);const nat=c.natCode||'';if(NAT[nat]?.[0])put(NAT[nat][0],c.id);for(const x of v689CountryAliases({country:c.id}))put(x,c.id);for(const x of V689_COUNTRY_ALIASES[c.id]||[])put(x,c.id);for(const x of V688_COUNTRY_TERMS[c.id]||[])put(x,c.id);for(const [alias,code] of Object.entries(NAT_ALIAS||{}))if(code===nat)put(alias,c.id);}
 return m;
}
function v691CountryId(raw,index){
 const k=v688Fold(raw);if(!k)return '';
 if(k==='congo')return C.some(c=>c.id==='cgo')?'cgo':'';if(k==='dr congo'||k.includes('democratic republic'))return C.some(c=>c.id==='cod')?'cod':'';
 const exact=index.get(k);if(exact?.size===1)return [...exact][0];
 let hit='';for(const [a,ids] of index){if(a.length<4||ids.size!==1)continue;if(k===a||k.includes(a)||a.includes(k)){const id=[...ids][0];if(hit&&hit!==id)return '';hit=id;}}
 return hit;
}

function v691ClubSimulation(country,division,name){
 const rows=WorldCatalog.clubs.filter(t=>t.country===country&&!String(t.id).startsWith('v691_'));
 const deepest=Math.max(1,...rows.map(t=>Number(t.division)||1)),ref=rows.filter(t=>Number(t.division)===deepest),avg=(a,k,d)=>a.length?a.reduce((n,x)=>n+(Number(x[k])||0),0)/a.length:d;
 const base0=avg(ref,'base',avg(rows,'base',62)),bal0=avg(ref,'balance',avg(rows,'balance',6500000)),cap0=avg(ref,'capacity',avg(rows,'capacity',12000)),j=(v691Hash(name)%5)-2,depth=Math.max(1,division-deepest);
 return {base:E.clamp(Math.round(base0-depth*3+j),42,82),balance:Math.max(250000,Math.round(bal0*Math.pow(.58,depth))),capacity:Math.max(2500,Math.round(cap0*Math.pow(.78,depth)))};
}

function v700PlayerScore(p,c){const a=v700NormName(p.name),b=v700NormName(c.name);if(!a||!b)return 0;let s=0;if(a===b)s=220;else if((a.includes(b)||b.includes(a))&&Math.min(a.length,b.length)>=6)s=168;else{const aa=a.split(' '),bb=b.split(' '),al=aa.at(-1),bl=bb.at(-1);if(al&&al===bl&&aa[0]?.[0]===bb[0]?.[0])s=160;}const pa=Number(p.age),ca=Number(c.age);if(pa&&ca)s+=Math.max(-25,18-Math.abs(pa-ca)*8);return s;}
function v700PickPlayer(p,cands){const r=(cands||[]).map(x=>({x,s:v700PlayerScore(p,x)})).filter(z=>z.s>=155).sort((a,b)=>b.s-a.s);if(!r.length)return null;if(r[1]&&r[0].s-r[1].s<8&&r[0].s<210)return null;return r[0].x;}

function v701MdText(x){return String(x||'').replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g,' ').replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g,'$1').replace(/[*_`>#]/g,' ').replace(/\bImage\b/gi,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}

  const pes=(section.match(/(?:PES\s*(?:ID)?\s*[:#]?\s*)(\d{3,})/i)||[])[1]||'',fifa=(section.match(/(?:FIFA|EA)\s*(?:ID)?\s*[:#]?\s*(\d{3,})/i)||[])[1]||'';
  out.push({name,age,ovr:overall,pac:v700Avg([speed,acc]),sho:v700Avg([finish,kick,oa]),pas:v700Avg([low,loft]),dri:v700Avg([ball,drib,tight]),def:v700Avg([da,tackle,de,agg]),phy:v700Avg([contact,stamina,jump,balance]),gkAwareness:gka,gkCatching:gkc,gkParrying:gkp,gkReflexes:gkr,gkReach:gkreach,pesId:pes,fifaId:fifa,pk});

v700UpdateAllRatings=async function(){
 if(v700RatingRun.active){toast('A atualizacao global de ratings ja esta em andamento.','warn');return;}
 Object.assign(v700RatingRun,{active:true,cancelled:false,phase:'Preparando',done:0,total:0,ea:0,efootball:0,kept:0,unmatched:0,sourceErrors:0,cached:0,errors:[],controllers:new Set(),startedAt:Date.now()});v700PaintStatus();
 try{
  const clubs=WorldCatalog.clubs.filter(t=>Array.isArray(rosterCache[t.id]?.players)&&rosterCache[t.id].players.length);if(!clubs.length){toast('Nenhum elenco salvo para atualizar ratings.','warn');return;}
  for(const t of clubs)for(const p of rosterCache[t.id]?.players||[])if(v700ExternalRef(p))v700RatingRun.kept++;
  v700SetPhase('Lendo cache FC27',1);await v701ApplyFcCache(clubs);v700RatingRun.done=1;v700PaintStatus();if(v700RatingRun.cancelled)return;
  v700SetPhase('Carregando indice PlayersDB',1);const teams=await v701LoadPdbTeams(v700RatingRun);v700RatingRun.done=1;v700PaintStatus();if(v700RatingRun.cancelled)return;
  await v701SourceSelfTest(teams);if(v700RatingRun.cancelled)return;
  const needs=clubs.filter(t=>t.id!=='t12'&&(rosterCache[t.id]?.players||[]).some(p=>!v700ExternalRef(p)));v700SetPhase('eFootball 2027 rapido · todos os clubes',needs.length);
  let cursor=0,sinceSave=0,saveChain=Promise.resolve();const checkpoint=()=>{sinceSave++;if(sinceSave>=40){sinceSave=0;saveChain=saveChain.then(()=>v700Checkpoint()).catch(e=>{v700RatingRun.sourceErrors++;v700RatingRun.errors.push('Checkpoint: '+e.message);});}};
  const worker=async()=>{while(cursor<needs.length&&!v700RatingRun.cancelled){const t=needs[cursor++];let applied=0;try{const pack=await v701FetchPdbClub(t,teams,v700RatingRun);if(!pack){v700RatingRun.unmatched++;}else{for(const p of rosterCache[t.id]?.players||[]){if(v700ExternalRef(p))continue;const hit=v701PickPlayer(p,pack.players);if(hit&&v700ApplyRef(p,v701Ref(hit,pack.pk))){applied++;v700RatingRun.efootball++;}}if(!applied)v700RatingRun.unmatched++;else v700RefreshEditorRatings(t.id);}}catch(e){if(!v700RatingRun.cancelled){v700RatingRun.sourceErrors++;if(v700RatingRun.errors.length<120)v700RatingRun.errors.push(t.name+': '+(e?.message||e));}}v700RatingRun.done++;checkpoint();if(v700RatingRun.done%5===0)v700PaintStatus();}};
  await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(20,Math.max(1,needs.length))},worker));await saveChain;if(!v700RatingRun.cancelled)await v700Checkpoint();
 }catch(e){if(!v700RatingRun.cancelled){v700RatingRun.sourceErrors++;v700RatingRun.errors.push(e?.message||String(e));toast(e?.message||String(e),'error');}}
 finally{for(const c of v700RatingRun.controllers)c.abort();const cancelled=v700RatingRun.cancelled,confirmed=(v700RatingRun.ea||0)+(v700RatingRun.efootball||0);v700RatingRun.active=false;v700RatingRun.phase=cancelled?'Interrompida':'Concluida';v700PaintStatus();if(!cancelled)toast(`Ratings concluidos: ${confirmed} novos · ${v700RatingRun.kept} ja existentes · ${v700RatingRun.unmatched} clubes sem match · ${v700RatingRun.sourceErrors} erros.`,confirmed?'success':'warn');try{refreshData?.();}catch(e){}}
};

async function v203RosterFromPlayersDb(team,teamsIndex,task){
 const pack=await v753FetchPdbClub(team,teamsIndex,task);if(!pack?.players?.length)throw Error('PlayersDB nao retornou elenco');const rows=[];
 for(const x of pack.players){const pos=v761Pos(x.pos),hit=v765InternalHit(x.name,pos),nationality=String(x.nationality||hit?.nation||''),age=Number(x.age||hit?.age)||0,ovr=Number(x.ovr||hit?.ovr)||0;if(!x.name||!['GOL','ZAG','LAT','VOL','MEI','ATA'].includes(pos)||!(ovr>=35&&ovr<=99)||!nationality||!(age>=14&&age<=55))continue;rows.push([x.name,pos,ovr,team.name,nationality,age]);}
 if(rows.length<V203_MIN_ROSTER||!rows.some(r=>v761Pos(r[1])==='GOL'))throw Error('PlayersDB nao forneceu 11 jogadores com OVR, idade, nacionalidade e posicao');v203EnrichInternalBase(rows);const item=v203BuildCompleteRoster(team,rows,{id:pack.pk,name:team.name},'PlayersDB · eFootball 2027');if(!item)throw Error('PlayersDB: elenco completo nao passou na validacao');return item;
}

async function v203EfootballRosterFor(team,teamsIndex,task){
 const errors=[];
 // 1) Cache/base interna enriquecida: nenhuma rede se ja houver 11 registros completos do clube.
 try{const direct=v765InternalClubRows(team,v765InternalClubGroupsCache),full=(direct||[]).filter(v203RowComplete);if(full.length>=V203_MIN_ROSTER&&full.some(r=>v761Pos(r[1])==='GOL')){const item=v203BuildCompleteRoster(team,full,{id:'internal',name:team.name},'Base eFootball interna completa');if(item)return item;}}catch(e){errors.push('base interna: '+(e?.message||e));}
 // 2) PESDB primeiro: e a fonte que entrega todos os campos necessarios na mesma linha.
 try{return await v203RosterFromPesdb(team,task,teamsIndex);}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;errors.push('PESDB: '+(e?.message||e));}
 // 3) PlayersDB somente se conseguir completar TODOS os campos essenciais.
 try{return await v203RosterFromPlayersDb(team,teamsIndex,task);}catch(e){if(task?.cancelled)throw e;errors.push('PlayersDB: '+(e?.message||e));}
 throw Error(errors.slice(0,5).join(' / ')||'nenhuma fonte eFootball retornou os cinco campos obrigatorios');
}

function v204CleanName(raw){

  .replace(/\s*\(TM\)\s*$/i,'')

}
function v204PosToken(x){

}

}
function v204ParseSelectedRoster(text){
 const src=String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,''),h=src.search(/Selected Roster\s*\(\s*\d+\s+players\s*\)[^\n]{0,120}eFootball/i);
 if(h<0)return [];
 const tail=src.slice(h),lines=tail.split('\n'),out=[],seen=new Set();let headers=0,started=false;
 for(const line of lines){
  if(/(?:^|\s)#?\s*\|\s*Pos\s*\|\s*Player Name\s*\|/i.test(line)){headers++;started=true;if(headers>1)break;continue;}
  if(started&&/^\s*#{3,6}\s+(?:Last Match|Recent Edits|Team Information|Roster Export Order)/i.test(line))break;
  if(!started||!line.includes('|'))continue;
  const c=v204Cells(line),pi=c.findIndex(v204PosToken);if(pi<0||pi+1>=c.length)continue;
  const rawPos=v204PosToken(c[pi]),rawName=c[pi+1];if(/GENERIC|SYSTEM/i.test(rawName))continue;
  const pes=(rawName.match(/PES\s*:\s*(\d{3,})/i)||[])[1]||'';
  const name=v204CleanName(rawName);let ovr=0;
  for(let i=c.length-1;i>pi+1;i--){const n=Number((c[i].match(/\b(\d{2})\b/)||[])[1]);if(n>=30&&n<=99){ovr=n;break;}}

  out.push({name,rawPos,pos:v753PdbPlayerPos(rawPos),ovr,pesId:pes});
 }

}
function v204ParseRosterAges(text){

function v94PackCurrentCareerArchive(s,year){

function writeCollection(token,key,value,limit){
  const isArray=Array.isArray(value),keys=[];let rows=[],idx=0,writes=0,pending=[];
  const commit=async(force=false)=>{
   if(!pending.length)return;const target=key==='players'?8:key==='allTimeStats'?8:6;if(!force&&pending.length<target)return;
   const batch=pending.splice(0,pending.length);await v18183DbWriteMany(batch);await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
  };
  const flush=async()=>{
   if(!rows.length)return;const raw=rows;rows=[];const payload=key==='players'?await v18191mEncodePlayerChunk(raw):raw,ck=shardKey(token,key,idx++);keys.push(ck);pending.push({key:ck,value:payload});writes++;
   if(key==='players')stats.playerChunksWritten++;
   if(v18121NewSeasonPreparation&&(key!=='players'||writes===1||writes%10===0)){v53Sim.progress=key==='players'?'Salvando jogadores compactados · bloco '+writes:'Salvando '+key+' da nova temporada';try{v53PaintStatus();}catch(e){}}
   await commit(false);
  };
  for(const id in (value||{})){rows.push([id,value[id]]);if(rows.length>=limit)await flush();}

function saveSharded(s,mode='full'){if(!s)return null;const t0=performance.now(),old=await rawRead(KEY),oldPrev=await rawRead(KEY+':previous'),token=[s.year,s.day,Date.now(),Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)].join('-'),set=new Set(SHARDS),root={};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(s))if(!set.has(k))root[k]=v;const collections={};
  // Libera a geracao mais antiga ANTES de escrever uma nova copia completa.
  // O checkpoint atual continua intacto ate o commit final, portanto uma falha
  // de gravacao nunca apaga a carreira valida que o usuario ja possuia.
  if(oldPrev?.format===FORMAT){
   try{
    await cleanupManifest(oldPrev,keysOf(old));
    await rawDelete(KEY+':previous');
   }catch(e){console.warn('[18.161] pre-limpeza de save antigo:',e?.message||e);}
  }
  const stableSeasonShards=new Set(['v538CareerArchive','v555CareerArchive','scorerArchive']);for(const key of SHARDS){const value=s[key];if(value==null)continue;const employmentReuse=mode==='employment'&&old?.format===FORMAT&&!['teams','coaches'].includes(key)&&old.collections?.[key],seasonReuse=old?.format===FORMAT&&Number(old.year)===Number(s.year)&&!s.seasonDone&&stableSeasonShards.has(key)&&old.collections?.[key];if(employmentReuse||seasonReuse){collections[key]=old.collections[key];continue;}const limit=key==='players'?900:key==='teams'?420:key==='coaches'?520:key==='schedule'?4:key==='competitionStats'?4:key==='allTimeStats'?700:(key==='v538CareerArchive'||key==='v555CareerArchive'||key==='scorerArchive'?1:120);collections[key]=await writeCollection(token,key,value,limit);} const meta={at:Date.now(),year:s.year,day:s.day,revision:Number(old?.revision||0)+1,format:FORMAT},manifest={format:FORMAT,version:1,revision:meta.revision,token,at:meta.at,year:s.year,day:s.day,root,collections};await commitManifest(manifest,old,meta);
  const keep=keysOf(manifest);for(const k of keysOf(old))keep.add(k);
  // Blocos de duas gerações atrás já não são referenciados por nenhuma versão
  // restaurável. A limpeza é pós-commit e não deve bloquear "Preparando férias".
  cleanupManifest(oldPrev,keep).catch(e=>console.warn('[17.1] limpeza de shards:',e));
  stats.lastSaveMs=Math.round(performance.now()-t0);stats.lastMode=mode;stats.lastHeapMiB=performance.memory?Math.round(performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize/1048576):null;if(mode==='employment')stats.employmentSaves++;else stats.fullSaves++;return {...meta,bytes:0,ms:stats.lastSaveMs,storage:'IndexedDB sharded (main)'};
 }

function seasonPayload(s,year){
  const archive=(s?.scorerArchive||[]).find(a=>Number(a?.year)===Number(year));
  const stats=[];
  for(const [competitionId,db] of Object.entries(archive?.competitions||{})){
   for(const [pid,row] of Object.entries(db||{})){
    stats.push({
     playerId:String(row?.id||pid),season:Number(year),competitionId:String(competitionId),clubId:null,
     apps:n(row?.apps),starts:0,minutes:n(row?.minutes),goals:n(row?.goals),assists:n(row?.assists),yellow:0,red:0
    });
   }
  }
  const clubStats=[];
  const packed=s?.v555CareerArchive?.[year];
  if(packed?.p){
   for(const pid of Object.keys(packed.p)){
    const rows=typeof v555PackedRows==='function'?v555PackedRows(s,year,pid):[];
    for(const r of rows)if(r?.teamId)clubStats.push({playerId:String(pid),season:Number(year),clubId:String(r.teamId),apps:n(r.apps),goals:n(r.goals),assists:n(r.assists),yellow:n(r.yellow),red:n(r.red)});
   }
  }else{
   for(const [pid,rows] of Object.entries(s?.v538CareerArchive?.[year]||{}))for(const r of rows||[])if(r?.teamId)clubStats.push({playerId:String(pid),season:Number(year),clubId:String(r.teamId),apps:n(r.apps),goals:n(r.goals),assists:n(r.assists),yellow:n(r.yellow),red:n(r.red)});
  }
    const transfers=(s?.transfers||[]).filter(x=>Number(x?.year)===Number(year)).map(x=>({
   playerId:String(x?.id||x?.playerId||''),season:Number(year),
   fromClubId:x?.from==null?null:String(x.from),toClubId:x?.to==null?null:String(x.to),
   day:Number.isFinite(Number(x?.day))?Number(x.day):null,fee:Number.isFinite(Number(x?.fee))?Number(x.fee):null
  })).filter(x=>x.playerId);
  const retirements=(s?.retirements||[]).filter(x=>Number(x?.year)===Number(year)).map(x=>({
   playerId:String(x?.id||x?.playerId||x||''),season:Number(year),
   day:Number.isFinite(Number(x?.day))?Number(x.day):null,clubId:x?.clubId==null?null:String(x.clubId)
  })).filter(x=>x.playerId&&x.playerId!=='[object Object]');
  return {season:Number(year),stats,clubStats,transfers,retirements};
 }

   const name=rawName||'Atleta';
   const natCode=raw.natCode??raw.nationality??player?.natCode??live?.natCode??'UNK';
   const mapKey=id||(v18203Norm(name)+'|'+natCode);
   let row=map.get(mapKey);
   if(!row){row={id,name,natCode,goals:0,assists:0,apps:0,minutes:0,clubs:{}};map.set(mapKey,row);}
   row.goals+=goals;row.assists+=assists;row.apps+=apps;row.minutes+=minutes;
   if(raw.clubs&&typeof raw.clubs==='object')Object.assign(row.clubs,raw.clubs);
   const clubName=raw.teamName??raw.clubName??raw.team?.name??raw.club?.name;
   if(clubName)row.clubs[String(raw.teamId??raw.clubId??Object.keys(row.clubs).length)]=clubName;
  };
  const walk=(node,key='',depth=0)=>{
   if(node==null||depth>6||typeof node!=='object')return;
   if(seen.has(node))return;seen.add(node);
   if(Array.isArray(node)){for(const item of node)walk(item,'',depth+1);return;}
   if(node instanceof Map){for(const [k,v] of node.entries())walk(v,String(k),depth+1);return;}
   const looks=('goals' in node)||('goal' in node)||('assists' in node)||('apps' in node)||('appearances' in node)||('matches' in node)||('minutes' in node)||('stats' in node);
   if(looks){merge(node,key);return;}
   for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(node))walk(v,k,depth+1);
  };
  const archives=Array.isArray(s.scorerArchive)?s.scorerArchive:[];
  if(selected==='all'){
   walk(s.allTimeStats||{});
   if(!map.size){for(const archive of archives)walk(archive?.competitions||archive);walk(s.competitionStats||{});}
  }else{
   const archived=archives.find(item=>Number(item?.year)===selected);
   const live=s.competitionStats||{};
   const liveRows=Object.values(live).some(value=>value&&typeof value==='object'&&Object.keys(value).length);
   if(selected!==Number(s.year)||(archived&&(!liveRows||s.seasonDone)))walk(archived?.competitions||archived||{});
   else walk(live);
  }
  try{
   const national=s.v640National?.playerStats;
   if(national){if(selected==='all')walk(national);else walk(national?.[String(selected)]||national?.[selected]||{});}
  }catch(e){}
  return [...map.values()].filter(row=>row.goals>0).sort((a,b)=>b.goals-a.goals||b.assists-a.assists||a.minutes-b.minutes||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'pt-BR'));
 }
